Conclusion: PersonaForge和PersonaForge-Bench共同为在真实多轮用户交互条件下训练和评估智能体奠定了基础。该工作填补了多轮智能体交互数据的空白，通过用户模拟框架实现了更贴近真实场景的数据合成与评估体系。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as agentic workflow executors, yet existing training data and benchmarks largely assume informationally complete, single-turn queries. Our analysis of 16K real-world sessions shows that 75.9% of interactions are multi-turn, revealing a substantial gap between how users interact with agents and how such systems are trained and evaluated. We introduce \textbf{PersonaForge}, a user simulation framework for synthesizing realistic multi-turn user--agent interactions. PersonaForge combines a four-dimensional persona space, SOUL-driven behavioral control calibrated to real-user statistics, and Reverse Deep Construction grounded in authentic seed queries. Using PersonaForge, we construct a 6.3K-record training dataset and \textbf{PersonaForge-Bench}, a manually annotated 138-task benchmark spanning over 20 professional domains with four-dimensional scoring. Experiments on Qwen3.5-27B show that PersonaForge training improves the composite score by +4.1%, with gains across all four dimensions and the largest improvements in Task Completion (+6.0%) and Response Quality (+6.8%). Further analyses show that PersonaForge-trained agents use fewer turns and tool calls, suggesting improved interaction efficiency, while ablations confirm the contribution of SOUL components and adaptive simulation. Together, PersonaForge and PersonaForge-Bench establish a foundation for training and evaluating agents under realistic multi-turn user interaction.

</details>


### [120] [When Linguistic and Internal Confidence Diverge in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28382)
*Hefan Zhang,Bingquan Zhang,Ming Cheng,Saeed Hassanpour,Weicheng Ma,Soroush Vosoughi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型（LLM）的"语言置信度"（即模型自我报告的置信度）是否与其内部置信度一致，发现二者存在显著的系统性偏差，语言置信度是一个有损信息通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户经常要求LLM报告自身的置信度，但尚不清楚这种语言层面的置信度表达是否反映了模型的内部置信状态。这一差距在下游可靠性应用中具有实际意义——如果模型的自我置信报告不可靠，则将其用于决策管道会带来风险。

Method: 跨8个分类任务、2个生成任务和3个模型家族的30个模型开展实验。分类任务沿三个轴（关联度、幅值一致性、校准度）比较语言置信度与基于logits的置信度；生成任务检验语言置信度是否追踪基于语义熵的不确定性。此外还包括提示词设计实验（态度提示词 vs 评分示例提示词）和回归分析以量化置信度分布特征对对齐模式的影响。

Result: 实例级关联度平均较弱，但在较简单样本上及较强基座模型上有所改善。指令微调模型倾向于报告更高置信度、有时关联度更高，但置信度差距更大、校准度更差。提示词设计主要改变报告置信度的分布形态；态度提示词会虚高置信度而不改善对齐；评分示例提示词在避免置信度坍缩时可保留排序信号。回归分析表明置信度评分的分布特征解释了大部分对齐模式，模型元数据在控制变量后的影响较小。

Conclusion: 结果支持"有损通道"假说：语言置信度虽然能携带一定排序信息（尤其当分布更分散时），但并不能保证校准。建议在使用语言置信度进行下游可靠性流水线之前，采用多维度诊断评估（关联度、幅值、校准度）而非仅依赖单一指标。

Abstract: Users often ask large language models (LLMs) to report how confident they are, but it is unclear whether such linguistic confidence tracks the model's internal confidence. We study this question across 8 classification tasks, 2 generation tasks and 30 models from three families. For classification, we compare linguistic confidence with logits-based confidence along three axes: association, magnitude agreement and calibration. For generation, we test whether linguistic confidence tracks semantic-entropy-based uncertainty. The axes frequently diverge. Instance-level association is weak on average, although it improves on easier items and for stronger base models. Instruction-tuned models often report higher confidence and sometimes show higher association, but they also have larger confidence gaps and worse calibration. Prompt design mostly changes the distribution of reported confidence. Attitude cues inflate confidence without improving alignment, while score exemplars can preserve rank-order signal when they avoid collapsed confidence values. Regression analyses show that distributional properties of confidence scores explain much of the observed alignment pattern, with model metadata playing a smaller role after controls. These results support a lossy-channel view of linguistic confidence. A more dispersed verbal confidence distribution can carry useful rank information, but it does not make the scores calibrated. Linguistic confidence should therefore be evaluated with multi-axis diagnostics before being used in downstream reliability pipelines.

</details>


### [121] [CultureConverse: A Multilingual Multi-turn Simulation Harness for Culturally Grounded Assistance in East and Southeast Asia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28405)
*Bryan Chen Zhengyu Tan,Weihua Zheng,Thong T. Doan,Bich Ngoc Doan,Jia Wang Peh,Xiaoyuan Yi,Jing Yao,Xing Xie,Nancy F. Chen,Zhengyuan Liu,JinYeong Bak,Wafi Shamdi,Soo Kai Chie,Liew Yu Siong,Aina Azyyati Binti Mohamad Rezal,Lew Yan Yan Vanessa,Huadan Wu,Dylan Raharja,Nadya Yuki Wangsajaya,Akane Fukushige,Kazushi Kato,Koji Inoue,Tatsuya Kawahara,Jaehyung Seo,Dongjun Kim,Seungyoon Lee,Zi Haur Pang,Rui Yang Tan,Charibeth Ko Cheng,Maria Regina Justina Estuar,Jann Railey Montalan,Pham Minh Duc,Roy Ka-Wei Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CultureConverse——一个可扩展的多语言文化场景对话模拟与评估框架，覆盖10个东亚及东南亚地区、58个子群体身份和7个领域，通过多轮对话评估LLM的文化感知助手能力，并发布了包含14,610条基准评估集和274,295条引导式训练集的CultureConverse-DS数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对大语言模型(LLM)的文化能力评估通常将其简化为单轮多选题式的事实回忆测试，无法捕捉真实使用场景中用户在多轮对话中寻求文化场景化帮助这一常见需求。此外，缺乏专门针对文化接地(culturally grounded)助手对话的系统性评估框架和多语言数据集。

Method: 构建了CultureConverse——一个自动化模拟与评估管线(harness)：(1)通过模拟用户与LLM助手的多轮对话，让助手在信息不完整的条件下从对话中推断文化约束并提供帮助；(2)覆盖10个东亚及东南亚地区、58个子群体身份和7个领域，确保文化多样性；(3)采用oracle-guided(gold-mode)模式生成高质量对话数据用于训练，同时生成基准(benchmark)模式数据用于评估；(4)使用18个模型进行基准评测，并通过人工标注实验验证评估框架与人类判断的一致性。

Result: (1)在18个模型的基准评估中，GPT-5 mini在协助质量上表现最优；(2)人工标注实验表明该评估框架是人类判断的充分代理(sufficient proxy)；(3)使用27,860条高质量CultureConverse-DS样本进行微调后，模型在域内文化协助任务上性能提升，且能力可迁移至文化多选题和文化安全分类等域外基准测试；(4)释放了完整的harness、数据集两个子集及评估judge prompts，支持文化能力的交互式评估。

Conclusion: CultureConverse证明了多轮文化场景对话是评估LLM文化能力的有效方式，弥补了现有单轮MCQ评估的不足。数据集和框架的公开将为社区提供评估和改进LLM文化素养的工具，同时微调实验表明该数据集不仅可用于评估，其训练数据也能有效提升模型的文化感知与安全性。

Abstract: Current cultural evaluations for large language models (LLMs) often reduce culture to single-turn factual recall via MCQs, failing to capture a common use case: users seeking practical help over multiple turns in culturally grounded scenarios. We introduce CultureConverse, a scalable, multilingual simulation and evaluation harness for culturally grounded assistant dialogue that covers 10 East and Southeast Asian regions, 58 subgroup identities, and 7 domains. Each simulated and evaluated episode produces a scored interaction where the assistant assists the user and infers cultural constraints from partial information. The resulting CultureConverse-DS dataset contains 14,610 benchmark (evaluation) episodes and 274,295 oracle-guided (gold-mode) dialogues. In our benchmark evaluation of 18 models, GPT-5 mini achieves the highest assistance quality. Human annotation experiments suggest that our evaluation framework is a sufficient proxy for human judgment. Performance gains from fine-tuning on 27,860 high-quality CultureConverse-DS samples improve in-domain assistance and transfer out-of-domain to cultural MCQ and safety classification benchmarks. We release the harness, both splits, and judge prompts to support interactive evaluation of cultural competency.

</details>


### [122] [A Unified Framework to Elicit Structured Feedback for Interpretable Multi-Trait Essay Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28407)
*Shihang Yang,Sanwoo Lee,Ningning Zhao,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 HiFTS，一个统一的自回归框架，用于多特征自动作文评分（AES）。该方法先生成层次化思维链（CoT）反馈，再预测特征级和整体分数，通过从教师 LLM 蒸馏层次化 CoT 反馈并联合训练学生模型同时生成反馈与评分，结合 GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）复合奖励优化评分一致性、校准、反馈质量和结构有效性，在推理时引入轻量全局先验减少长推理漂移。同时构建了中文多特征数据集 CFMS-34（951篇作文，34个评分标准特征），实验表明 HiFTS 在整体与特征级评分上表现优异，且生成连贯且符合评分标准的反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多特征自动作文评分（Multi-trait AES）需要在相互依存的多维特征间进行基于评分标准（rubric）的推理，而非孤立预测各个分数。现有反馈增强方法通常将反馈生成与评分解耦，或独立评估各特征，导致评分与反馈之间一致性弱、与评分标准的对齐不足。

Method: 1. **HiFTS 框架**：统一的自回归生成框架，先生成层次化 CoT 反馈，再预测特征级与整体分数。
2. **知识蒸馏**：从教师 LLM 中蒸馏层次化的 rubric-grounded CoT 反馈，训练学生模型联合生成反馈和评分。
3. **强化学习优化**：采用 Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)，以复合奖励函数平衡评分一致性、校准性、反馈质量与结构有效性四个维度。
4. **推理时全局先验**：引入轻量全局先验（global prior），为推理过程提供整体指导，减少长链推理中的漂移现象。
5. **CFMS-34 数据集**：构建中文多特征 AES 数据集，包含 951 篇作文、整体分数标注及 34 个基于评分标准的特征标签。

Result: 在 CFMS-34 和 ASAP++ 两个数据集上的实验表明，HiFTS 在整体评分和特征级评分上均取得优异性能，同时生成的反馈具有连贯性和与评分标准（rubric）的良好对齐，验证了统一框架在评分-反馈一致性方面的优势。

Conclusion: HiFTS 通过将层次化思维链反馈生成与多特征评分统一于单一自回归框架中，有效解决了现有方法中反馈与评分解耦、特征独立评估导致的评分-反馈一致性差和 rubric 对齐不足的问题。配合 GRPO 多目标奖励优化和推理时全局先验机制，实现了评分准确性与反馈质量的协同提升。CFMS-34 数据集的发布也为中文多特征 AES 研究提供了新的基准资源。

Abstract: Multi-trait Automated Essay Scoring (AES) requires rubric-grounded reasoning across interdependent traits, rather than isolated score prediction. Existing feedback-enhanced methods often decouple feedback from scoring or assess traits independently, weakening score--feedback consistency and rubric alignment. We propose HiFTS, a unified autoregressive framework that generates hierarchical CoT feedback before predicting trait-level and holistic scores. HiFTS distills rubric-grounded hierarchical CoT feedback from a teacher LLM and trains student models to jointly generate feedback and scores. HiFTS further applies Group Relative Policy Optimization with a composite reward balancing score agreement, calibration, feedback quality, and structural validity. At inference, a lightweight global prior provides holistic guidance to reduce drift during long-form reasoning. We also introduce CFMS-34, a Chinese multi-trait AES dataset with 951 essays annotated with holistic scores and 34 rubric-based traits. Experiments on CFMS-34 and ASAP++ show that HiFTS achieves strong holistic and trait-level scoring while producing coherent, rubric-aligned feedback.

</details>


### [123] [Are These Modules Worth Their Cost? A Paradigm-Level Accuracy-Cost Analysis of In-context Learning Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28432)
*Jiayan Lin,Yujia Liu,Zijin Hong,Zheng Yuan,Yilin Xiao,Hao Chen,Qinggang Zhang,Xiao Huang,Feiran Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在统一可控实现下对ICL文本到SQL流水线的17种范式级配置进行了系统性的成本-准确率量化分析，发现执行反馈精炼是唯一普遍有效且成本低的范式，并提出了可迁移的分级配置指导原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL文本到SQL研究通常仅报告端到端的整体执行准确率，未量化各设计选择（模块）对准确率的边际贡献及其成本代价，导致无法在范式层面进行统一的成本-准确率权衡理解与流水线配置优化。

Method: 研究者在单一可控实现下，围绕ICL文本到SQL流水线的5个核心模块实例化了17种范式级配置，在4种具备不同能力水平和推理风格的骨干模型上进行评估，逐一归因每种范式的边际准确率贡献及对应的Token成本（输入与输出分别核算），并进行跨模块堆叠组合分析与预算分配策略比较。

Result: 1. 执行反馈精炼（execution-feedback refinement）是唯一在所有骨干模型上普遍有效且成本稳定的范式；其余模块仅在骨干模型依赖的条件下有帮助。2. 输入Token需求主要受流水线结构影响，输出Token需求则对骨干模型的生成行为更敏感。3. 模块堆叠在多数骨干模型上提升准确率，但增益的叠加方式随骨干能力而变化。4. 在固定预算下，将资源投入于中等模型上的复杂流水线，往往优于将预算用于升级到前沿模型但使用精简流水线。5. 所得分级指导原则可零样本迁移到5个额外骨干模型，无需逐范式搜索。

Conclusion: 本文通过系统性、可复现的范式级消融实验，填补了ICL文本到SQL领域成本-准确率量化的空白，揭示了流水线设计中模块效应的高度骨干依赖性和执行反馈的独特通用性，并提供了可迁移的、预算感知的分级配置指南，为工程实践中在准确率与推理成本之间的实际权衡提供了可操作依据。

Abstract: Recent advances in in-context learning (ICL) text-to-SQL have substantially improved execution accuracy on public benchmarks by assembling increasingly elaborate pipelines around the base generator, yet existing studies typically report aggregate end-to-end accuracy, without quantifying the marginal accuracy-cost contribution of individual design choices. Consequently, providing a unified, paradigm-level cost-accuracy quantification remains a critical challenge for understanding and configuring modern text-to-SQL. To address this, we instantiate 17 paradigm-level configurations across five recurring modules of the ICL text-to-SQL pipeline under a single controlled implementation, and attribute each paradigm's marginal contribution and incurred cost across all four backbones spanning diverse capability levels and reasoning styles. Our analysis reveals that execution-feedback refinement is the only paradigm whose benefit holds universally at consistently low cost, while most other modules help only under backbone-dependent conditions. Token accounting shows that input demand is more closely tied to pipeline structure, whereas output demand is more sensitive to backbone generation behavior. Cross-module analysis further shows that stacking improves accuracy on most backbones, although how the gains compose varies with backbone capability. We also find that a fixed budget is often better spent engineering a more elaborate pipeline over a mid-tier backbone than upgrading to a frontier model with a lean pipeline. These findings distill into an actionable, cost-aware tiered guideline that transfers to five additional backbones without per-paradigm search.

</details>


### [124] [Fidelity Is Not Enough: Dispatch-Level Instrumentation for Agentic Datasheet Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28439)
*Qing Ye,Meng-Hsuan Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Fidelity（提取值与来源的匹配度）作为智能体文档提取的标准度量存在根本盲区：一个模型因结构化输出约束静默禁用了工具调用，却在未读取数据表的情况下凭虚构来源文本通过所有保真度检查。作者构建了37条手工标注声明的智能体基准，通过记录每次工具调用日志，开发了两件工具：规则式失败归因分类器与"静默失败检测器"（仅检查调用了哪些工具，从不检查提取值）。检测器在207次干净运行上零误报、在50个人工植入故障上全召回，但两者并不对称——前者界定了假阳性率，后者是构造性的召回率，而对"调用了工具却仍答错"的检测力未测量。工具层的价值在于可移植性与可观测性而非准确性，只有当文档超出上下文窗口时其溢价才被挣回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者在为内部提取服务进行模型资质审查时发现：一个结构化输出约束静默禁用了工具使用，但模型仍以虚构来源文本作答，而该运行通过了所有保真度检查。这一发现暴露了当前智能体文档提取评估范式的核心缺陷——保真度仅度量提取值与来源的匹配，却完全不验证执行过程（工具是否被正确调用），导致静默失败（silent failure）无法被现有度量捕获。作者由此立题：能否构建一种仅基于过程信号（工具调用记录）而非结果信号（提取值）的检测机制？

Method: 1. **基准构建**：设计一个智能体基准，包含37条手工标注声明，分布在四个组件上（25条覆盖三个组件，12条覆盖第四个组件），并记录每次工具调用。
2. **仪器开发**：从工具调度记录构建两件工具：
   - 规则式失败归因分类器（rule-based failure-attribution classifier）
   - 静默失败检测器（silent-failure detector）：两条规则仅检查调用了哪些工具，从不检查提取值。
3. **验证实验**：
   - 在三个模型家族的207次干净保真度通过提取上运行检测器，检验假阳性率。
   - 植入50个人工故障（恰好撤回检测器所检查的工具），检验召回率。
4. **第二独立预言机**：构建因果实验舱（causal chamber），通过物理测量检验数据表声明，但故意设计为部分性（仅验证37条声明中的2条），并给出其余声明无法物理分级的分类学。
5. **受控扰动实验**：在三种已部署模型栈（其中一个被其服务栈而非能力差距所不稳定化）上，施加受控扰动，观察保真度与实验舱判决的行为差异。

Result: - **假阳性率**：检测器在207次干净运行上零误报，界定假阳性率上界为0。
- **召回率**：检测器召回全部50个人工植入故障（召回率由构造性保证为100%）。
- **非对称性揭示**：两项结果不对称——假阳性率是经验界定的，召回率是构造性的；对"调用了工具却仍答错"的检测力未测量（unknown detection power）。
- **因果实验舱**：在受控扰动下，保真度全程通过，而实验舱判决恰好在测量不确定度处翻转，说明两者检测的是不同维度的失败。
- **工具层价值定位**：工具层购买的是可移植性与可观测性而非准确性；其溢价只有在文档超出上下文窗口时才能被挣回。
- **实验舱部分性**：37条声明中仅2条可被物理验证，其余给出无法分级原因的完整分类学。

Conclusion: 保真度作为智能体文档提取的唯一度量存在结构性盲区，无法捕获因工具配置问题导致的静默失败。仅基于过程信号（工具调用记录）的检测机制可以在不检查提取值的情况下识别出此类故障，但其检测力对"工具调用正确但结果错误"的场景仍不可测，暴露了纯过程监控的局限性。因果实验舱作为独立预言机验证了过程监控与结果保真度检测的是不同维度。工具层的实际价值在于为模型部署提供可移植性与可观测性，而非直接提升准确性——其投入产出比与文档规模是否超出上下文窗口正相关。论文的核心方法论贡献在于：提出将工具调用日志作为一等评估信号，并明确区分了假阳性率与召回率的非对称验证逻辑。

Abstract: One model passed our fidelity check without ever opening the datasheet. We found it while qualifying models for an internal extraction service: a structured-output constraint had silently disabled tool use, and the model answered anyway, with fabricated source text. Only the per-tool trace exposed it. Fidelity -- whether an extracted value matches the source -- is the standard measure for agentic document extraction, and it scores that run a success. We therefore log every tool call in an agentic benchmark of 25 hand-curated claims over three components, with 12 more on a fourth, 37 in all. From that dispatch record we build two instruments: a rule-based failure-attribution classifier, and a silent-failure detector whose two rules check only which tools were called, never the extracted value. The detector raises no flag on 207 clean fidelity-passing extractions across three model families, and recovers all 50 planted faults that withhold exactly the tools its rules check. The two results are not symmetric: the first bounds the false-positive rate, the second is recall by construction, and detection power against runs that call their tools and still answer wrongly is unmeasured. A second, independent oracle, a causal chamber that tests whether the datasheet's claims hold under physical measurement, is intentionally partial: it confirms only what the apparatus can exercise, a verifiable envelope of 2 of those 37 claims, and we give a taxonomy of why the rest are not physically gradable. Under a controlled perturbation, fidelity passes throughout while the chamber verdict flips exactly at the measurement uncertainty. Across three deployed model stacks (one destabilised by its serving stack, not by any capability gap) the tool layer buys portability and observability rather than accuracy, and earns its premium only once a document outgrows the context window.

</details>


### [125] [Sliding-window beats linear attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28444)
*Alexia Jolicoeur-Martineau,Rhea Sanjay Sukthanker,Pashmina Cameron,Emy Gervais*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文证明基于"注意力汇聚点"（attention sinks）的滑动窗口注意力（SWA）在性能上与甚至优于经过后期训练的线性注意力（Linear Attention）模型，同时SWA无需后期训练、推理速度极快且内存占用极低，是当前解决LLM二次复杂度问题更廉价且可靠的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型因二次方注意力机制导致内存和能耗随上下文长度急剧增长——每生成一个新token，所有历史键（Keys）和值（Values）都需无限期存储在内存中，这在实践中不可持续。线性注意力被提出作为解决方案以突破二次复杂度瓶颈，但该研究方向一直缺乏与更简单基线（如滑动窗口注意力）的充分对比。

Method: 作者将基于注意力汇聚点（sinks）的滑动窗口注意力（SWA）与经过后期训练的线性注意力模型进行系统对比实验。实验覆盖多个LLM架构和多种下游任务，特别关注长上下文推理任务（包括Needle-in-a-Haystack和BABILong基准测试），从性能、推理速度和内存消耗三个维度进行评估。

Result: 1. **总体性能**：SWA在多种LLM和下游任务上表现与后期训练的线性注意力模型持平或更优。2. **长上下文推理**：在Needle-in-a-Haystack和BABILong任务上，SWA性能大幅领先线性注意力模型，达到2至10倍的性能提升。3. **效率优势**：SWA无需后期训练（post-training）、推理速度极快、内存占用极低，使其成为一个极其廉价且可靠的解决方案。

Conclusion: 作者强烈建议：若目标是降低推理内存成本，应优先选择SWA而非对线性注意力模型进行后期训练。线性注意力模型虽然展示了潜在前景，但要达到与SWA相当的性能水平，可能需要从零开始训练或进行大量后期训练，这使其实际性价比远不如SWA。本文揭示了线性注意力研究缺乏简单基线对比的问题，并表明SWA是解决二次复杂度问题的高性价比实用方案。

Abstract: Due to the nature of quadratic attention, Large Language Models (LLMs) consume a lot of memory and energy. Every new token costs more than the previous one. For each additional token, the keys and values must be stored in memory indefinitely, which is unsustainable.
  Several alternatives have been proposed to fix the quadratic scaling problem, one of which is retrofitting LLMs to use Linear Attention. This idea has attracted a lot of attention, given its promise to solve the quadratic scaling problem with state-of-the-art performance at low cost. However, this line of research has not been properly compared to simpler baselines.
  In this work, we show that Sliding Window Attention (SWA) with sinks performs as well or better than post-trained Linear Attention models. We observe this across multiple LLMs on various downstream tasks. For long-context reasoning tasks (Needle-in-a-Haystack and BABILong), SWA achieves massively higher performance (2 to 10 times higher than linear attention). SWA requires no post-training, is extremely fast, and requires low memory; therefore, making it an extremely cheap and reliable solution.
  To reduce inference memory cost, we strongly recommend switching to SWA instead of post-training linear models. Linear attention models may have shown some promise, but they likely require to be trained from scratch or extensive post-training in order to even match SWA.

</details>


### [126] [Acquire, Repair, Preserve: A Diagnosis-Guided Post-Training Recipe for Small-Model Dialogue Game Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28458)
*Nan Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文针对 LM Playschool Challenge 的交互式对话博弈场景，使用一个 2B 开源权重模型，提出"广泛 SFT 打基础 → 回合级偏好对修复可机械验证的局部决策失误 → 保持静态能力"的三步训练配方。官方最终评测中公共 clemscore 从 10.67 提升至 38.92，闭源域内分数从 13.41 提升至 41.17，同时静态聚合性能基本持平（44.14 vs. 基线 44.24）。跨域 clemscore 仅 7.88，增益主要集中在目标博弈家族的未见变体上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态基准（static benchmarks）大多隐含测试模型的能力，而交互式对话博弈显式要求模型跨轮次维护状态、解释反馈、并在变化约束下做出有效动作。作者以 LM Playschool Challenge 为检验平台，发现 2B 开源模型的失败类型并不局限于广义知识缺失，还包括大量"局部决策失败"：重复猜测、动作格式错误、以及违反模型刚刚看到过的反馈信息。这些可机械验证的诊断结果，为设计有针对性的训练配方提供了依据。

Method: 训练配方分为三步：1）通过监督微调（SFT）获得广泛的博弈参与能力，作为主要的能力来源；2）针对单一对话博弈家族，使用回合级（turn-local）偏好对（preference pairs）来修复可机械验证的局部决策失败，例如重复猜测和格式错误；3）在获得上述增益的同时保持模型在这些对话博弈之外的通用静态能力。方法强调失败检测的精确性是回合级监督能否有效的前提条件。

Result: 在 LM Playschool 官方最终评测中，提交模型将公共 clemscore 从 10.67 提升至 38.92，闭源域内（closed in-domain）分数从 13.41 提升至 41.17；聚合静态性能与基线基本持平（44.14 对比基线 44.24），说明未牺牲通用能力。跨域（out-of-domain）clemscore 仍较低，仅 7.88。增益在未见过的目标博弈家族变体上最为集中，跨家族迁移能力有限。

Conclusion: 对于小规模开源模型在交互式对话博弈上的表现，广义 SFT 贡献了大部分能力提升；回合级（turn-local）监督在失败检测精确的前提下是有效的补充手段，但观察到的能力迁移主要局限于目标博弈家族内部，跨家族泛化仍是一个明显的瓶颈。这提示在交互式博弈训练中，应区分"知识层面"与"局部决策层面"的失败，并分别设计训练信号。

Abstract: Interactive dialogue games test a capability that static benchmarks largely leave implicit: a model must carry state across turns, interpret feedback, and choose valid actions under changing constraints. We study this setting in the LM Playschool Challenge with a 2B open-weight model, and find that many failures are not only broad knowledge failures but also local decision failures: repeated guesses, malformed actions, and violations of feedback that the model has just seen. These diagnostics motivate a training recipe organized around three steps: acquire broad game participation through supervised fine-tuning, repair mechanically verifiable failures within one targeted dialogue-game family using turn-local preference pairs, and preserve general capabilities beyond these dialogue games. In the official final evaluation, our submission improves public clemscore from 10.67 to 38.92 and closed in-domain score from 13.41 to 41.17, while approximately preserving aggregate static performance (44.14 vs. 44.24 for the baseline). Out-of-domain clemscore remains low at 7.88, with the largest gains concentrated in unseen variants of the targeted family. Our results suggest that broad SFT brings most of the model's capability improvement; turn-local supervision can be effective when failure detection is precise, with observed transfer concentrated primarily within-family.

</details>


### [127] [Stranger, Fan, or Peer? A Systematic Study on the Role of Interlocutor in Persona-Based Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28467)
*Daniela Occhipinti,Malvina Nissim,Marco Guerini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于人设的对话系统中，说话人传记信息在训练、推理和评估三个阶段可见性的因素分解问题。研究发现：训练时的可见性（而非推理时）决定了模型是否通过对话表达人设特征还是直接复制传记文本；有对话方传记可见性的模型比没有的更少复制目标传记；在非对称披露条件下（仅对话方看到目标传记），目标内容更容易泄漏到对话方发言中，且包含此类痕迹的对话更容易被LLM评委识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于人设的对话系统通常需要说话人的传记作为条件，但对话至少涉及两个参与者，且谁能在何时访问谁的传记在训练、推理和评估中可以变化。以往工作往往忽视了这些方面，将这些因素视为单一因素处理，而没有将它们分解为训练、推理和评估三个独立阶段的可见性配置。这掩盖了只有单独切换传记可见性时才出现的机制。

Method: 研究人员在配对有说话人传记的对话数据集上，通过变量控制实验，分别改变目标说话人和对话方在训练和推理阶段是否能看到对方传记（三阶段因素分解），并使用大语言模型（LLM）作为评委执行作者识别任务，以检测传记内容的泄漏情况。具体评估包括：(1) 模型是否表达人设特征 vs 复制传记文本；(2) 目标传记文本的复制程度；(3) 在非对称披露条件下目标内容向对话方发言的泄漏程度及评委识别难度。

Result: 1. **训练时可见性起决定性作用**：训练时的传记可见性（而非推理时）决定了模型是否通过对话表达人设特征，还是退化为直接复制传记文本（这是基于人设生成中的已知问题/现象）。
2. **对话方传记可见性减少目标传记复制**：在训练中有对话方传记可见性的模型比没有的更少复制目标传记文本；而仅在推理时改变可见性的效果则不太一致。
3. **非对称披露导致泄漏**：当仅对话方看到目标传记（非对称披露）时，目标内容更频繁地泄漏到对话方的发言中；包含此类痕迹的对话更容易被LLM评委识别，尤其是当对话方发言可见时。

Conclusion: 传记内容泄漏到生成的对话轮次中，是一种由对话方可见性在训练和推理中如何配置所产生的产物。将训练、推理和评估三个阶段分离开来进行分析是必要的，以往将其视为单一因素的做法是不充分的。这一发现对于设计和评估基于人设的对话系统具有重要方法论意义。

Abstract: Persona-based dialogue systems are usually conditioned on speaker biography, but dialogues involve at least two participants, and who has access to whose biography can vary across training, inference, and evaluation. Prior work often neglected these aspects, obscuring mechanisms that only appear when biography visibility is toggled separately across training, inference, and evaluation, a three-stage factorisation that prior work has largely treated as a single factor. We study this factorisation on a dataset of dialogues paired with speaker's biographies, varying whether the target and interlocutor speakers see each other's biographies during training and inference, and using an LLM as a judge to perform author identification. We find that (i) training-time visibility, more than inference-time visibility, determines whether models express persona traits through dialogue or fall back on copying biographical text (a known problem/phenomenon in persona-based generation); (ii) models trained with interlocutor-biography visibility copy less target-biographical text than models trained without it, while changing visibility only at inference time has a less consistent effect; and (iii) under asymmetric disclosure, where only the interlocutor sees the target biography, target content leaks into interlocutor turns more often, and dialogues containing such traces are easier for the judge to identify, especially when interlocutor turns are visible. These results suggest that biography leakage into generated turns is an artefact of how interlocutor visibility is configured across training and inference, and separating the three stages is necessary.

</details>


### [128] [ContextPilot: Teaching Agents for Proactive Context Management via Fine-grained RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28476)
*Zhuoshi Pan,Qizhi Pei,Junru Lu,Honglin Lin,H. Vicky Zhao,Di Yin,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ContextPilot 通过扩展上下文管理工具集（加入规划、长期记忆、软上下文卸载）并提出面向上下文管理的细粒度强化学习方法，有效提升了长周期智能体推理任务中的上下文管理效率与最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期智能体任务要求LLM在多轮交互中持续检索、整合和维持分散信息，但保留全部交互历史会导致工作上下文无限增长。现有的主动上下文管理方法存在三大局限：(1) 工具集有限，仅支持检索、删除和摘要，缺乏全局规划、长期记忆和自适应压缩能力；(2) 探索效率低下，对所有上下文管理动作一视同仁，忽视了它们对最终结果的异质性影响；(3) 信用分配粒度过粗，将轨迹级别的奖励平均分配给所有中间上下文编辑动作，导致RL训练信号模糊。

Method: 1. **工具集系统性扩展**：在原有检索/删除/摘要工具基础上，新增三类工具：规划工具（global planning）、长期记忆工具（long-term memory）、软上下文卸载工具（soft context offloading / adaptive compression）。
2. **面向上下文管理的RL方法**：
   - **关键决策识别**：利用上下文变化量（context variation）和熵变化（entropy variation）来识别对后续推理轨迹影响最大的"关键编辑决策"，仅对关键决策进行分支采样（branch sampling），提高探索效率。
   - **动作级优势估计**：从所有经过对应上下文编辑动作的分支轨迹中，估计该动作的优势值（action-level advantage），实现细粒度信用分配，替代传统的轨迹级奖励平均分配。

Result: 在长上下文问答（long-context QA）和深度搜索（deep search）任务上的实验表明：ContextPilot 在保持更紧凑工作上下文的同时实现了更强的性能，跨多种基础模型和基准测试均一致优于现有基线方法。代码已开源：https://github.com/Tencent/ContextPilot

Conclusion: ContextPilot 通过系统性扩充上下文管理工具集和引入细粒度RL信用分配机制，有效解决了现有主动上下文管理方法在工具能力、探索效率和信用分配粒度上的三大瓶颈，证明了"工具扩展+精准RL"的联合优化范式对长周期智能体推理任务的显著增益。

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic tasks require large language models (LLMs) to iteratively retrieve, integrate, and maintain dispersed information across multi-turn interactions, but preserving all interaction histories leads to a continuously growing working context. Recent proactive context management methods allow models to edit their own working context with specialized tools, yet they still face three key limitations: (1) a limited toolset restricted to search, deletion, and summarization, with no support for global planning, long-term memory, and adaptive compression; (2) inefficient exploration that treats context management actions uniformly despite their heterogeneous impacts on final outcomes; and (3) coarse-grained credit assignment that assigns the final trajectory-level reward to all intermediate context editing actions during RL. To bridge these gaps, we introduce ContextPilot, a proactive context management framework for long-horizon agentic reasoning. Our approach systematically augments the toolset with planning, long-term memory, and soft context offloading tools. We further propose an RL method tailored for context management, which uses context and entropy variation to identify critical editing decisions for branch sampling and estimates action-level advantages from all branched trajectories that pass through the corresponding context editing action. Experiments on long-context QA and deep search tasks show that ContextPilot achieves stronger performance with a more compact working context, consistently outperforming existing baselines across various base models and benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/Tencent/ContextPilot.

</details>


### [129] [Blind Men and the Elephant: Probing the Epistemic Myopia of LLMs under Long-Tail Divergent Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28478)
*Zhuoshi Pan,Junru Lu,Yan Qian,H. Vicky Zhao,Di Yin,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ElephantBench 是一个包含1,094道问题的闭卷知识探针基准，通过可审计的图基流水线从低曝光网页语料中提取自然存在的分歧事实，构建"多账号"问答记录，用于评估大语言模型是否能同时保留长尾事实的多个不同版本（而非仅记住唯一标准答案）。在32个模型的测试中，即使是表现最好的模型也仅在52.4%的问题上同时恢复了两个账号，揭示了LLM参数化记忆中存在显著的"认知近视"（epistemic myopia）现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事实性QA基准通常假设每个问题存在唯一的标准答案，这掩盖了一个关键问题：LLM在参数化记忆中是否保留了关于长尾事实的多重分歧叙事。当训练语料中某一版本因曝光频率更高而占据主导地位时，模型可能丢失少数派版本的认知，形成认知偏差。然而，这一"认知近视"问题缺乏系统性的诊断工具。

Method: 研究构建了一条可审计的图基流水线，具体步骤如下：(1)从低曝光的网页语料中检索相关文档；(2)自动识别文档间自然存在的分歧（disagreements）；(3)将分歧转化为多账号QA记录，每个答案均可溯源至原始文档；(4)通过原始文档和权威公共网络源对答案进行交叉验证；(5)由人工标注员进行质量审核。最终生成1,094道问题的闭卷知识探针（ElephantBench），并在32个模型上进行横向测试。此外，研究还进行了语料层面分析，考察曝光不平衡与多账号召回之间的关系。

Result: (1)即使是性能最强的模型，也仅在52.4%的问题上同时恢复了两个账号；在几乎所有其余问题上，模型仅召回其中一个账号而遗漏另一个，呈现系统性不完整。(2)扩大模型规模和增加推理时计算（inference-time reasoning）虽能改善召回率，但无法消除这种不完整现象。(3)语料分析表明，曝光不平衡有利于主导账号的保留，而增加少数派版本的曝光与更完整的召回率正相关。

Conclusion: ElephantBench 确立为诊断参数化记忆中认知近视的可复现知识探针。其核心贡献不仅是基准本身，更在于提供了一种基于图的、高效且可扩展的基准构建流水线，可将长尾语料转化为可溯源的知识探针。这一方法论为评估和推进下一代LLM的"认知严谨性"（epistemic rigour）提供了系统性框架，也为未来从语料曝光多样性角度提升模型知识保真度指明了方向。代码已公开于 GitHub（Tencent/ElephantBench）。

Abstract: Factual question answering (QA) typically assumes a single canonical answer, obscuring whether large language models (LLMs) retain divergent accounts of long-tail facts. To address this gap, we introduce ElephantBench, a closed-book knowledge probe comprising 1,094 questions generated through an auditable graph-based pipeline. The pipeline retrieves related documents from a low-exposure web corpus, identifies naturally occurring disagreements, and converts them into multi-account QA records. Each answer is verified against the originating documents and authoritative public web sources and is then reviewed by human annotators. Across 32 models, even the strongest model recovers both accounts on only 52.4% of questions, while on nearly all remaining questions it recalls one account but omits the other. Scaling model size and inference-time reasoning improve recall but do not eliminate this incompleteness. Corpus analysis further shows that exposure imbalance favors the dominant account, whereas greater minority-side exposure is associated with more complete recall. These findings establish ElephantBench as a reproducible knowledge probe for diagnosing epistemic myopia in parametric memory. More broadly, our graph-based benchmark construction pipeline provides an efficient and scalable way to turn long-tail corpora into source-traceable knowledge probes, supporting efforts to evaluate and advance the epistemic rigour of next-generation LLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/Tencent/ElephantBench.

</details>


### [130] [NL2AGBench: Benchmarking LLM Auto-Formalization for AlphaGeometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28481)
*Samuel Xiao,Judy Song,Rory Hu,Ziliang Zong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 NL2AGBench 基准，用于评估大语言模型（LLMs）将自然语言几何题翻译为 AlphaGeometry 专用语言（DSL）的能力。通过对10个主流LLM的评测，发现闭源模型执行翻译率超过80%，而开源模型表现显著不足；同时提出了语法与逻辑错误分类体系及多种缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在自然语言理解和数学推理方面已取得显著进展，但将非正式数学问题（尤其是几何题）转化为形式化表示的能力仍研究不足。这一局限对神经符号几何系统（如 AlphaGeometry）尤为关键：其定理证明引擎依赖特定领域专用语言（DSL）作为输入，但手动将自然语言题目转换为 AlphaGeometry 形式化语法存在严重的使用瓶颈。因此需要系统性地评估和提升 LLM 在此翻译任务上的能力。

Method: 1. **基准构建**：设计 NL2AGBench，专门评估 LLM 将英文几何问题翻译为 AlphaGeometry 兼容形式化表示的能力。
2. **验证机制**：采用基于执行（execution-based verification）的验证方式——即在实际 AlphaGeometry 引擎中运行翻译结果来检验正确性，而非仅依赖文本相似度。
3. **评测范围**：覆盖10个最先进的开源与闭源 LLM，涵盖不同参数规模，从可执行翻译准确率、语法正确性、错误特征三个维度进行分析。
4. **错误分类体系**：引入错误分类法（error taxonomy），区分语法错误（syntax errors）与逻辑错误（logic errors）。
5. **缓解策略探索**：研究少样本提示（few-shot prompting）、微调（fine-tuning）以及人工引导提示（human-guided hinting）等多种方法在不同模型家族上的效果。

Result: 1. **性能差距显著**：闭源与开源模型之间存在巨大性能鸿沟——领先闭源模型的可执行翻译率超过80%，而即便是最大的开源模型也难以一致地保持几何约束并生成有效形式化表示。
2. **错误类型分析**：通过错误分类体系揭示了语法错误与逻辑错误的分布特征。
3. **缓解策略有效**：少样本提示、微调及人工引导提示等多种策略在不同模型家族上均带来了可测量的性能提升。

Conclusion: NL2AGBench 填补了 LLM 在自然语言到几何形式化翻译任务上系统性评估的空白。研究结果表明，当前开源模型在几何问题形式化翻译方面仍有较大提升空间，而闭源模型已展现出实用级能力。所提出的错误分类体系和缓解策略为后续改进 LLM 在神经符号几何系统中的翻译能力提供了明确的改进方向和方法论基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in natural language understanding and mathematical reasoning. However, their ability to translate informal mathematical problems into formal representations remains underexplored. This limitation is particularly important for neuro-symbolic geometry systems such as AlphaGeometry, whose theorem-proving engine requires inputs in a specialized domain-specific language (DSL). Although AlphaGeometry achieves near-IMO gold-medalist performance, manually converting natural-language problems into its formal syntax remains a significant usability bottleneck. To address this challenge, we introduce the Natural Language to AlphaGeometry Benchmark (NL2AGBench), which evaluates LLMs in translating English geometry problems into AlphaGeometry-compatible formal representations. NL2AGBench uses execution-based verification within AlphaGeometry to assess translation quality rather than relying solely on textual similarity. We evaluate ten state-of-the-art open- and closed-source LLMs across multiple parameter scales and analyze executable translation accuracy, syntactic correctness, and error characteristics. Our experiments reveal a substantial performance gap between closed- and open-source models: leading closed-source models achieve executable translation rates above 80%, while even the largest open-source models struggle to consistently preserve geometric constraints and produce valid formalizations. We introduce an error taxonomy distinguishing syntax and logic errors and investigate mitigation strategies, including few-shot prompting, fine-tuning, and human-guided hinting, which yield measurable improvements across multiple model families.

</details>


### [131] [Ladders in Chaos: When, How, (and Perhaps Why) Does Test-Time Scaling Improve LLM Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28496)
*Di Wu,Sergey Troshin,Christof Monz,Antske Fokkens,Vlad Niculae*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在机器翻译任务中两种测试时缩放范式——序列式采样与并行式采样——的性能特性与机制差异。发现序列采样在性能上限和样本多样性方面优于并行采样，尤其在低预算场景下优势明显；人工评估表明序列采样显著提升翻译流畅度与自然度，但高预算下可能降低准确性；机制分析将其成功归因于模型能利用更大的目标侧上下文，并通过消融实验验证了其对温度的鲁棒性以及对上下文构建方式的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在测试时缩放（test-time scaling）方面已形成两种主流范式：序列式（后续答案依赖于先前答案）和并行式（如i.i.d.采样配合重排序）。在机器翻译领域，这两种范式的各自特性及其对翻译质量的影响机制尚不明确。研究旨在阐明二者的性能差异、适用条件及底层机制，以指导翻译场景中测试时计算资源的有效分配。

Method: 1. 对序列采样与并行采样（i.i.d. 采样 + 重排序）在翻译任务中的性能进行对比实验，考察不同采样预算下的表现差异；
2. 通过多维度的手动人工分析，对Best-of-N翻译结果从流畅度、自然度、准确性等维度进行标注与评估；
3. 设计受控实验，分析序列自改进成功的内在机制，特别考察模型对更大目标侧上下文的访问能力的作用；
4. 进行消融实验，测试序列采样在不同采样温度下的鲁棒性，以及对上下文构建方式的敏感性。

Result: 1. 序列采样具有更高的性能上限，尤其在较小采样预算下能提供更多样且有效的候选样本池；
2. 人工分析表明：序列采样显著提升了翻译的流畅度与自然度，但在推理预算较大时可能损害翻译准确性；
3. 受控分析将序列自改进的成功部分归因于模型能够访问更大的目标侧上下文；
4. 消融实验显示序列采样对不同采样温度具有鲁棒性，但对上下文构建方式较为敏感。

Conclusion: 序列采样通过赋予模型更大的目标侧上下文窗口而有效提升了机器翻译质量，其优势在较小采样预算下尤为显著。该方法对采样温度的设置具有鲁棒性，但对上下文的构建方式较为敏感，提示了未来工作的改进方向——即通过优化上下文构建策略来进一步提升序列采样的性能上限。

Abstract: Two forms of test-time scaling for Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as effective and widely adopted paradigms: sequential, in which later answer attempts depend on earlier ones, and parallel, such as i.i.d. sampling with reranking. In this study, we investigate their properties in translation. First, our study shows that sequential sampling has a higher performance ceiling, providing a more diverse and effective pool of samples, particularly under smaller sampling budgets. Second, we interrogate the nature of test-time scaling through a multidimensional manual analysis. Human analysis of the Best-of-$N$ translations demonstrates that sequential sampling substantially improves translation fluency and naturalness, but can degrade accuracy when inference budgets are large. Finally, we suggest an explanation of the mechanism through which sequential scaling improves machine translation. Our controlled analysis partially attributes the success of sequential self-improvement to the model's access to a larger target-side context. Ablation experiments on sequential sampling demonstrate its robustness across different sampling temperatures, while also revealing sensitivity to context construction, suggesting directions for future improvement.

</details>


### [132] [Phoneme- and Word-Level Metrics Using Self-Supervised Speech Representations for Forced Alignment Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28508)
*V. S. D. S. Mahesh Akavarapu,Michael Daniel,Gerhard Jäger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两个基于自监督（SSL）语音表征的无参考强制对齐评估指标——音素聚类互信息（PCMI）和词语声学一致性分数（WACS），用于克服传统强制对齐评估依赖人工标注时间戳的限制，实现大规模、多语言的自动对齐质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强制对齐（forced alignment）的质量评估通常需要人工标注的时间戳作为参考，这严重限制了评估的大规模化和多语言覆盖能力。在低资源语言或新语言上获取高质量人工标注成本极高，亟需无需参考标注即可自动评估对齐质量的方法。

Method: 提出两种基于SSL语音表征的语料级（corpus-level）评估指标：
1. **PCMI（Phoneme-Cluster Mutual Information）**：计算对齐后的音素标签与从SSL语音表征诱导出的聚类之间的互信息，衡量音素标签与语音表征聚类的吻合程度。
2. **WACS（Word Acoustic Consistency Score）**：利用动态时间规整（DTW）相似性，度量同一词语在不同实例中SSL语音表征序列的一致性，反映词级对齐的声学一致性。
实验设计包括：使用随机和系统性扰动验证指标的敏感性；在FLEURS的85种语言上跨多种对齐系统进行评估；在DoReCo的45种语言上与人工标注对齐进行验证；并在两种语音学复杂的低资源语言上进行测试。

Result: - 在随机和系统性对齐扰动下，PCMI和WACS均呈现一致的性能退化趋势，验证了指标的有效性。
- 跨85种语言、多种对齐系统的评估表明，两种指标能有效区分高质量与低质量的对齐结果。
- 与基于时间戳的对齐质量度量具有强相关性，在DoReCo的45种语言上与人工标注对齐的结果一致。
- 在两种低资源、语音学复杂的语言上同样表现良好，证明了方法对低资源场景的适用性。

Conclusion: 本文证明基于SSL语音表征的无参考评估方法能够替代传统依赖人工标注的强制对齐质量评估，具有可扩展性强、无需参考时间戳、支持多语言和低资源语言的优势。PCMI和WACS两个指标互补，分别从音素级和词级提供对齐质量评估能力。相关代码已开源为Python包，便于社区使用和进一步研究。该工作为推动语音技术在全球语言和场景下的应用提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Forced alignment evaluation typically requires manually annotated timestamps, limiting large-scale and multilingual analysis. We introduce two corpus-level metrics based on self-supervised (SSL) speech representations for reference-free forced alignment evaluation: Phoneme-Cluster Mutual Information (PCMI) and Word Acoustic Consistency Score (WACS). PCMI measures agreement between aligned phoneme labels and clusters induced from SSL-speech representations, while WACS measures consistency of repeated word realizations using dynamic time warping similarity between word representation sequences. Using both random and systematic perturbations, we show that PCMI and WACS degrade consistently under alignment perturbations. We further analyze the metrics across multiple alignment systems on 85 languages from FLEURS, validate them against manually annotated alignments from 45 languages in DoReCo, and evaluate them on two phonologically complex low-resource languages. The metrics effectively separate high- and low-quality alignments and correlate strongly with timestamp-based alignment quality measures. Our results demonstrate that SSL-speech representations enable scalable, reference-free forced alignment evaluation. The metrics are available as an open-source Python package at https://github.com/mahesh-ak/forced-aligner-metrics.

</details>


### [133] [A Formal Limitation on Learning Human Language From Textual Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28560)
*Emily Cheng,Ryan Cotterell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文用信息论方法证明：仅凭话语形式恢复说话人意图的能力存在理论不可逾越的上界——该上界由语言形式本身留下的"意义不确定性"决定（分为不可约减部分和仅靠语境可消解的部分），任何表征方法（包括大语言模型隐状态）都无法突破这些上界。在人工语言、中文零代词消解和颜色指称任务上得到实证支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类语言和NLP领域的核心问题是：仅凭话语形式能否完全恢复说话人的意图？尽管大语言模型等表征手段不断进步，但语言中的歧义和指代问题是否可以从根本上被消除？该论文旨在从信息论角度给出一个根本性的理论回答：存在不可逾越的上界，且这些上界是语言内在属性而非表征方法的缺陷。

Method: 1. 将语言使用建模为对意义（meanings）、语境（contexts）和话语（utterances）的联合分布。
2. 基于任意文本特征提取器（包括当代大语言模型的隐状态）作为"听众"表征，从信息论角度推导解码器从话语表征恢复说话人意图的概率上界。
3. 将形式对意义的剩余不确定性分解为两部分：（a）不可约减部分（irreducible）——仅凭语言形式永远无法消除的歧义；（b）仅可通过超语言语境（extralinguistic context）解析的部分——话语本身永远无法解决，但上下文可以消除的歧义。
4. 证明这些上界对离散或连续的意义空间均成立，且独立于表征的复杂度或训练数据量。
5. 通过三类实验进行实证验证：人工语言（artificial languages）、普通话零代词解析（Mandarin zero-pronoun resolution）、颜色指称任务（color reference）。

Result: 1. 推导出解码器恢复说话人意图概率的信息论上界，该上界由形式对意义的剩余不确定性（irreducible + context-only）共同决定。
2. 证明上界对任意表征方法（包括LLM隐状态）和任意意义空间（离散/连续）均成立。
3. 实证结果在人工语言、普通话零代词解析、颜色指称三个任务上均验证了理论预测：即使使用强大的LLM表征，恢复意图的概率仍被理论上限所约束，未能超越该上界。

Conclusion: 语言形式本身蕴含的信息存在不可逾越的理论天花板。无论使用何种编码器或表征方法，解码器从话语表征中恢复说话人意图的上界完全由语言本身的内在不确定性决定，而非由模型能力决定。这一发现对理解人类语言交流的本质及设计自然语言处理系统的预期具有深远意义。

Abstract: Can a listener recover what a speaker means from the form of an utterance alone? We answer this question information-theoretically, and for a listener given by any featurizer of text, including the hidden states of contemporary large language models. Modeling language use as a joint distribution over meanings, contexts, and utterances, we derive upper bounds on the probability that a decoder recovers a speaker's intended meaning from a representation of the utterance. The bounds are governed by the uncertainty that form leaves about meaning, which splits into an irreducible part and a part that only (extralinguistic) context, but never the utterance alone, can resolve. Because these quantities are intrinsic to language, no representation, however much text or supervision produced it, can surpass them; the bounds hold whether the space of meanings is discrete or continuous. Experiments on artificial languages, Mandarin zero-pronoun resolution, and color reference provide empirical evidence in support of the theory.

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Joint User Association and Pilot Assignment via Phase-Shifted Pilots for Scalable Cell-Free mMIMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27614)
*Mumtaz Ozlen,Ahmet Sacid Sumer,Ahmed Naeem,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自适应相位偏移(APS)导频设计的联合用户关联(UA)与导频分配(PA)框架，用于解决无蜂窝大规模MIMO(CF-mMIMO)中的导频污染问题，使AP可同时区分的UE数量受子载波数而非正交导频序列数限制，调度UE数量较传统交错(CI)方案翻倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CF-mMIMO通过为所有UE提供均匀服务来实现广域覆盖，但其性能依赖准确的信道估计。信道估计基于正交导频序列，而正交导频序列的数量受限于信道相干块长度。当UE密度增大时，导频序列不可避免地复用，导致导频污染，严重限制了系统的可扩展性。

Method: 1. **APS导频设计**：所有UE共享一个全带宽导频信号，通过为每个UE在频域施加独立的相位偏移，使其信道冲激响应(CIR)在AP处的时域上落在连续且互不重叠的区间内，从而在时域上实现UE分离。AP可分离的UE数量由此由子载波数决定，而非受限于正交导频序列数。2. **联合UA与PA框架**：将用户关联问题建模为0/1背包问题——物品重量为UE的信道抽头数(taps)，背包容量为可用子载波数。3. **公平性增强机制**：在候选链路的价值评估中，对UE已有连接数量施加惩罚项，以提升系统公平性。

Result: 1. **UE调度数量**：较传统交错(CI)基准方案提升了超过一倍。2. **信道估计精度(MSE)**：在总导频功率相等时，MSE与CI基准相当；在每子载波功率相等时，MSE低于CI基准。3. **连接质量与公平性**：5百分位连接性能得到改善，Jain公平指数显著提升，有效缓解了边缘用户的性能退化。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将UE分离能力从正交导频序列数解耦至子载波数，从根本上突破了传统导频污染对CF-mMIMO系统可扩展性的制约。APS导频设计在保持或提升信道估计精度的同时，显著增加了可调度的UE数量，并通过公平性惩罚机制改善了边缘用户体验，为高密度用户场景下的CF-mMIMO系统提供了一种高效可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Cell-free massive multiple-input multiple-output (CF-mMIMO) promises uniform service across the coverage area, and this service relies on accurate channel estimation for every user equipment (UE). These estimates are obtained from orthogonal pilot sequences, whose number is bounded by the channel coherence block. As a result, the sequences are reused as the UE density increases and the resulting pilot contamination limits the scalability. This paper proposes a joint user association (UA) and pilot assignment (PA) framework built on the adaptive phase-shifted (APS) pilot design, in which all UEs share a single full-band pilot and a per-UE phase shift applied in the frequency domain places their channel impulse responses (CIRs) in contiguous, non-overlapping intervals of the time domain at the access point (AP). The number of UEs that an AP can separate is thus set by the number of subcarriers rather than by the number of orthogonal sequences, and the UA is formulated as a $\mathbf{0/1}$ knapsack problem whose item weights are the numbers of taps and whose capacity is the number of subcarriers. Analysis and simulations show that the proposed design more than doubles the number of UEs scheduled by the conventional interleaved (CI) benchmark. It attains the same mean-squared error (MSE) as the CI benchmark under an equal total pilot power, and a lower MSE under an equal per-subcarrier power. It further improves the 5th-percentile connectivity and Jain's fairness index by penalizing the number of existing connections of a UE in the value assigned to each candidate link.

</details>


### [135] [Secure Pseudonymetry with DSSS Watermarking for LEO Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27693)
*Nisanur Camuzcu,Alireza Vahid*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低功率直接序列扩频（DSSS）水印框架，将可恢复的卫星伪名标识符和基于密钥的HMAC-SHA256认证字段嵌入到LEO下行链路信号中，使接收端无需完整解调下行信号即可被动检测、识别和验证卫星，同时不影响传统接收机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨（LEO）星座为定位、导航与授时（PNT）提供了密集的信号机会（SOP），但被动使用这些信号面临三大挑战：（1）信标/导频分量微弱；（2）多颗卫星信号重叠干扰；（3）接收机无法可靠识别观测值来自哪颗卫星。现有方案无法在不做完整下行解调的情况下实现卫星身份识别与信号认证，因此需要一种轻量级、被动的标识与认证机制。

Method: 1. **DSSS水印嵌入**：设计低功率直接序列扩频水印，将其嵌入LEO下行链路信号中，水印功率远低于主信号，避免对原始下行传输造成显著影响。
2. **水印内容结构**：包含两部分——（a）公开的卫星伪名（pseudonym），用于接收端被动识别信号来源卫星；（b）基于密钥的HMAC-SHA256认证字段，供授权方进行完整性与真实性验证。
3. **被动检测流程**：接收端仅需截获下行信号即可检测水印、恢复卫星公开ID并验证认证字段，无需完整解调下行链路内容。
4. **多星仿真评估**：利用Starlink两行轨道根数（TLE）推导的几何关系、多普勒频移及链路预算缩放，结合Sionna RT射线追踪传播模型进行本地传播仿真，评估多星干扰下的水印性能。
5. **向后兼容设计**：水印框架对不处理水印的传统接收机完全透明，不影响既有LEO系统正常工作。

Result: 1. 在多星共观干扰场景下，候选卫星检测功能有效——接收端能够从重叠信号中正确识别出携带水印的卫星。
2. 公开卫星伪名可成功恢复——实现了卫星身份的正确解析。
3. 基于密钥的HMAC-SHA256认证验证通过——授权验证机制在多星干扰下仍然可靠。
4. 水印对模拟信标/导频及主下行链路分量的扰动有限——低功率设计保证了原始信号质量不受显著影响。
5. 框架验证了在不依赖完整下行解调的前提下实现被动检测、识别与认证的技术可行性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的低功率DSSS水印框架为LEO被动PNT提供了一种实用的卫星标识与认证解决方案。通过在下行信号中嵌入伪名ID和HMAC认证字段，接收端可在不执行完整解调的情况下实现被动检测、卫星识别和信号验证，有效应对多星信号重叠和信标微弱等挑战。该方案对传统接收机完全透明，兼容性好，适用于未来支持发射端水印注入的LEO系统，为无需专用导航基础设施的定位、导航与授时技术开辟了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Low-Earth-orbit (LEO) constellations offer dense signals of opportunity (SOP) for positioning, navigation, and timing (PNT) without dedicated navigation infrastructure. However, using these signals passively, by only listening to the downlink, is difficult when beacon/pilot components are weak, signals from multiple satellites overlap, or the receiver cannot reliably identify which satellite generated a given observable. This paper proposes a low-power direct-sequence spread-spectrum (DSSS) watermarking framework that embeds a recoverable pseudonymous satellite identifier and keyed authentication field into a LEO downlink signal. The watermark carries a public satellite pseudonym for identification and a keyed HMAC-SHA256-based authentication field for authorized verification, enabling passive detection and verification without full downlink demodulation. We evaluate the framework in a multi-satellite simulation using Starlink two-line-element (TLE)-derived geometry, Doppler, and link-budget scaling with Sionna RT-based local propagation. The results demonstrate candidate-satellite detection, public-ID recovery, and keyed verification under co-observed satellite interference, with limited perturbation to the simulated beacon/pilot and primary downlink components. The framework targets future transmitter-enabled LEO systems while remaining transparent to legacy receivers that do not process the low-power watermark.

</details>


### [136] [Data-Aided Asynchronous OFDM Integrated Sensing and Communications: A Mean-Field Variational Bayes Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27739)
*Van-Chung Luu,Nuria González Prelcic,Duy H. N,Nguyen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向异步上行OFDM-ISAC系统的数据辅助变分贝叶斯(VB)框架，在联合处理定时偏差(TO)和载波频率偏差(CFO)的同时，利用已检测的数据符号辅助感知参数估计，显著提升了通信与感知联合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际上行ISAC系统中，定时偏差(TO)和载波频率偏差(CFO)会在子载波和OFDM符号间引入相位失真，严重降低数据检测精度和感知参数估计性能。同时，传统方法依赖较多导频开销，限制了有效感知资源的利用。因此，亟需一种同步感知(data-aided)且无需额外导频开销的接收机设计方案。

Method: 1. **信号建模**：将接收信号建模为稀疏多径叠加模型，联合推断发送数据符号、复数路径增益、空间频率、时延-多普勒参数及同步参数。
2. **均值场VB算法**：
   - 对角度和时延-多普勒相位参数采用 **von Mises分布** 进行无网格(gridless)更新；
   - 采用 **Gamma-Gaussian先验** 促进路径稀疏性。
3. **数据辅助感知机制**：在初始导频辅助估计后，将已检测的数据符号作为额外观测，进一步精细化信道与感知参数估计，从而在不增加导频开销的前提下增加等效感知资源。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法在以下指标上均优于SAGE、SBL、AB2FM及仅导频辅助VB基线：
- **符号错误率(SER)** 更低；
- **信道重建精度** 更高；
- **路径参数估计** 更准确；
- **TO/CFO估计** 性能更优；
- **3D定位精度** 显著提升。

此外，忽略TO和CFO会导致感知性能严重退化，验证了同步感知设计的必要性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了同步感知(考虑TO/CFO)与数据辅助接收机设计对ISAC系统的重要性。所提数据辅助VB框架在无需额外导频开销的条件下，通过复用已检测数据符号辅助感知估计，在通信和感知性能上均取得了显著提升，为未来上行异步ISAC系统的接收机设计提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) is regarded as a key technology for sixth-generation wireless networks, allowing sensing and communication operations to jointly utilize the same spectrum and hardware infrastructure. However, in practical uplink ISAC systems, timing offset (TO) and carrier-frequency offset (CFO) introduce phase distortions across subcarriers and OFDM symbols, which can severely degrade both data detection and sensing-parameter estimation. In this paper, we propose a data-aided variational Bayesian (VB) framework for asynchronous uplink OFDM-ISAC systems. Specifically, the received signal is modeled as a sparse multipath superposition, where the transmitted data symbols, complex path gains, spatial frequencies, delay-Doppler parameters, and synchronization parameters are jointly inferred. To enable tractable inference, we develop a mean-field VB algorithm in which von Mises distributions are used for gridless updates of the angular and delay-Doppler phase parameters, while a Gamma-Gaussian prior is adopted to promote path sparsity. A key feature of the proposed framework is its data-aided sensing capability: after initial pilot-based estimation, the detected data symbols are exploited as additional observations to refine the channel and sensing parameters. This substantially increases the effective sensing resources without requiring extra pilot overhead. The simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves superior performance compared with SAGE, SBL, AB2FM, and pilot-only VB baselines in terms of symbol error rate, channel reconstruction accuracy, path-parameter estimation, TO/CFO estimation, and 3D localization accuracy. The results also demonstrate that ignoring TO and CFO leads to severe sensing degradation, highlighting the importance of synchronization-aware and data-aided receiver design for ISAC systems.

</details>


### [137] [Fast Tri-Hybrid Beamforming via Deep Unfolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27759)
*Pinjun Zheng,Md. Jahangir Hossain,Anas Chaaban*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于动态超表面天线(DMA)的多用户下行系统中三混合(tri-hybrid)波束成形快速框架，通过将迭代算法与图神经网络(GNN)展开技术相结合，在保证系统吞吐率的同时将运行时间降低了超过一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三混合多输入多输出(MIMO)架构为下一代无线系统提供了增强的设计灵活性，且不会按比例增加硬件成本或功耗。然而，由此产生的三重域耦合(射频域、基带域和超表面域)使得波束成形优化变得极其困难且计算量大，阻碍了实时部署。

Method: 1. 利用加权速率最大化与加权最小均方误差(MMSE)最小化之间的等价性，在逐DMA输入功率约束下推导了一种迭代算法，所有更新方程均有闭式解，但收敛速度天然较慢；
2. 为实现实时操作，将该迭代算法展开为一个可训练的有限迭代架构，采用图神经网络(GNN)以确保置换等变性(permutation equivariance)，并支持可变用户数；
3. 在射线追踪(ray-tracing)信道数据上进行训练。

Result: 展开方法在系统总吞吐率上达到与迭代算法相当或更高的性能，同时将运行时间减少了超过一个数量级。该方法在不同环境中展现出强可扩展性、鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于GNN展开的三混合波束成形框架有效解决了DMA系统中三重域耦合优化带来的计算瓶颈，实现了高性能与实时性的统一，为下一代基于DMA的无线系统提供了实用化的波束成形解决方案。

Abstract: Tri-hybrid multiple-input multiple-output architectures have recently emerged as a promising enabler for next-generation wireless systems, as they potentially provide enhanced design flexibility without a proportional increase in hardware cost or power consumption. However, the resulting triple-domain coupling renders beamforming optimization challenging and computationally demanding. This paper develops a fast tri-hybrid beamforming framework for multiuser downlink systems employing dynamic metasurface antennas (DMAs). Based on the equivalence between weighted sum-rate maximization and weighted sum-minimum mean square error minimization, an iterative algorithm is first derived under per-DMA input power constraints, with all update equations available in closed form, but convergence inherently remains slow. To enable real-time operation, the algorithm is further unfolded into a trainable finite-iteration architecture using graph neural networks that ensure permutation equivariance and support varying numbers of users. Trained on ray-tracing channel data, the unfolded method achieves comparable or higher system sum-rates while reducing runtime by more than an order of magnitude. The method also demonstrates strong scalability, robustness, and generalization across various environments.

</details>


### [138] [Robust Joint Beamforming and Configuration Design in FARIS-Aided Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27835)
*Hong-Bae Jeon,Yonghwi Kim,Hyung-Joo Moon,Kai-Kit Wong*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于流体有源可重构智能表面（FARIS）辅助的多用户系统的鲁棒传输设计方案，在不完美信道状态信息（CSI）条件下，通过WMMSE重构与交替优化（AO）框架联合优化基站波束赋形、FARIS系数及活跃元件选择，实现最小加权和速率最大化，并在仿真中验证了优于传统设计方案的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FARIS 同时具备有源反射和动态端口选择能力，可为多用户系统提供增强的灵活性和自由度。然而，实际部署中 CSI 不完美是一个关键挑战，传统的优化方案通常假设完美 CSI，导致在现实信道条件下性能退化。因此，需要在显式考虑 CSI 误差的前提下，设计鲁棒的传输方案以充分利用 FARIS 的有源放大能力和端口选择自由度。

Method: 1. **问题建模**：将系统建模为鲁棒最小加权和速率最大化问题，联合优化基站波束赋形向量、FARIS 利用率系数和有源元件选择变量，同时显式纳入 CSI 误差界和实际功率约束。

2. **问题难点**：由于耦合优化变量以及离散的端口选择结构，原始问题本质上是非凸的。

3. **WMMSE 重构**：首先通过加权最小均方误差（WMMSE）方法将原始速率最大化问题转化为等效的二次型问题，从而解耦部分变量并简化优化结构。

4. **交替优化（AO）框架**：设计 AO 算法，将问题分解为若干子问题逐一求解——包括波束赋形子问题、FARIS 系数优化子问题和离散端口选择子问题。每个子问题均具有高效的闭式或近似解，整体算法保证收敛到平稳点。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的鲁棒 FARIS 方案在不同 CSI 误差水平下均持续优于传统设计方案（如基于完美 CSI 的非鲁棒方案或无有源放大/端口选择的方案），验证了在 CSI 不确定性条件下联合利用自由度增强（DoF enhancement）与有源信号放大能力的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的鲁棒 FARIS 辅助多用户传输方案有效应对了实际场景中 CSI 不完美的问题，通过 WMMSE 与 AO 的组合方法将复杂非凸问题转化为可高效求解的子问题序列。实验证明该方案能够在保持系统稳定性的同时显著提升多用户加权和速率，展现了 FARIS 在有源反射和动态端口选择方面的独特优势，为实际部署提供了可行的设计参考。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a robust transmission design for multi-user systems assisted by a fluid active reconfigurable intelligent surface (FARIS), which enables both active reflection and dynamic port selection and thereby offers enhanced flexibility, under imperfect channel state information (CSI). We formulate a robust minimum sum-rate maximization problem by jointly optimizing the base station beamformer, the utilized FARIS coefficients, and the active element selection, while explicitly accounting for CSI errors and practical power constraints. The resulting problem is inherently nonconvex due to the coupled optimization variables and discrete port-selection structure. To tackle this challenge, we first reformulate the original problem via a weighted minimum mean square error (WMMSE) approach and then devise an alternating optimization (AO) framework, where each resulting subproblem admits efficient solutions and the overall algorithm converges to a stationary point. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed robust FARIS scheme consistently outperforms conventional designs, highlighting the effectiveness of jointly leveraging degree-of-freedom (DoF) enhancement and active signal amplification under CSI uncertainty.

</details>


### [139] [Enabling Secure Wireless Communications for FARIS-Aided Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27837)
*Hong-Bae Jeon,Yonghwi Kim,Hyung-Joo Moon,Kai-Kit Wong*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了由流体型有源可重构智能表面（FARIS）辅助的 securely 下行传输问题，通过联合优化发射波束成形、有源反射系数和流体端口配置来最大化 secrecy rate，并提出了交替优化框架求解，实验表明 FARIS 在安全通信中具有显著优势和对不利窃听几何的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理层安全（Physical-layer Security）是现代无线通信面临的重要挑战，尤其在窃听者几何位置不利的情况下，传统有源或无源智能反射面（RIS）的灵活性有限。RIS 通常只能通过被动反射调控信号，无法主动放大信号或动态选择工作端口，在复杂环境中安全性能受限。本文旨在引入 FARIS 技术，结合有源反射和流体端口动态选择，提升物理层安全能力，为困难场景下的安全通信提供新方案。

Method: 1. 问题建模：建立 secrecy rate 最大化问题，联合优化三个变量：(a) 发射波束成形器（transmit beamformer），(b) 有源反射系数（active reflection coefficients），(c) 流体端口配置（fluid port configuration），在满足实际功率约束的条件下进行优化。
2. 求解框架：由于原问题高度非凸，提出定制的交替优化（Alternating Optimization, AO）框架，将联合设计分解为多个可处理的子问题，每个子问题在保证系统约束的前提下都能高效求解。
3. 迭代流程：交替迭代优化波束成形、反射系数和端口配置，逐步收敛到局部最优解，实现波束成形与 FARIS 重构的有效联合优化。

Result: 数值结果表明：(1) 所提出的 FARIS 辅助设计在 secrecy rate 上持续优于现有基准方法（benchmarks）；(2) FARIS 在面对不利的窃听几何条件（unfavorable eavesdropping geometries）时展现出良好的鲁棒性，说明其适用于复杂、挑战性环境中的安全通信场景。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入流体型有源可重构智能表面（FARIS），将主动反射和端口动态选择结合，有效提升了物理层安全传输性能。所提交替优化框架能够高效解决联合优化问题，实验证实 FARIS 相较传统方案具有明显性能优势且对窃听几何鲁棒，展现了 FARIS 作为挑战性环境下安全通信强大使能技术的潜力。

Abstract: This paper investigates secure downlink transmission assisted by a fluid active reconfigurable intelligent surface (FARIS), which enables both active reflection and dynamic port selection, offering enhanced flexibility for physical-layer security. We formulate a secrecy rate maximization problem that jointly optimizes the transmit beamformer, active reflection coefficients, and fluid port configuration under practical power constraints. To efficiently handle the resulting highly nonconvex problem, we develop a tailored alternating optimization (AO) framework that decomposes the original joint design into tractable subproblems, where each admits an efficient solution while preserving the system constraints, enabling an effective joint optimization of beamforming and FARIS reconfiguration. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed FARIS-assisted design consistently outperforms the benchmarks. The results further highlight the robustness of FARIS against unfavorable eavesdropping geometries, confirming its potential as a powerful enabler for secure communications in challenging environments.

</details>


### [140] [Toward Secure Communications for a UAV Swarm with Movable Antennas in SAGIN: CKM-Enabled Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27973)
*Jiayang Wan,Yafei Wang,Jiawei Zhuang,Wenjin Wang,Tony Q. S. Quek*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对星-空-地一体化网络（SAGIN）下的无人机安全下行通信问题，提出了一种基于信道知识地图（CKM）辅助的多智能体强化学习框架，联合优化可移动天线（MA）阵列位置、无人机轨迹与链路选择，以最大化无人机集群的保密能效（SEE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. **窃听威胁**：空-地对地链路通常由视距（LoS）传播主导，无线信道的广播特性使其极易遭受被动窃听，物理层安全性亟待提升。
2. **能效与安全协同**：需要在保障保密通信性能的同时，最大化系统能效，避免以高能耗为代价换取安全性。
3. **实时信道状态信息（CSI）获取开销大**：大规模无人机集群与多维链路选择下，实时CSI获取成本过高。
4. **联合优化的高维动作空间**：MA位置、轨迹与链路选择的联合优化导致动作空间维度爆炸，计算复杂度高。
5. **局部决策与系统全局性能对齐困难**：多智能体各自优化易导致局部最优，需协调机制保证系统级约束满足。

Method: ### 系统模型
- **网络架构**：SAGIN使能的无人机集群，无人机可在卫星网络、空中网络（中继无人机）和地面网络之间选择服务链路。
- **物理层安全增强**：引入可移动天线（MA）阵列，通过调整天线位置充分利用连接性与空间自由度提升保密性能。
- **优化目标**：最大化无人机集群的保密能效（SEE），联合优化MA位置、无人机轨迹及链路选择。
- **约束条件**：无人机移动性约束、MA移动约束、链路连通性约束。

### 核心技术方案

#### 1. CKM辅助的CSI获取
- **信道知识地图（CKM）构建**：基于稀疏信道测量数据，采用Kriging插值方法构建CKM。
- **高效CSI存取**：将CKM与卫星星历信息结合，实现CSI的高效存储与按需检索，大幅降低实时CSI获取开销。

#### 2. 低维混合动作空间设计
- **刚体运动学建模**：将MA阵列建模为刚体，通过全局刚体平移（而非独立移动各天线单元）调整位置。
- **动作空间降维**：将MA位置优化从逐元素移动简化为整体平移，构造低维混合动作空间，降低计算复杂度。

#### 3. 多智能体强化学习框架
- **个体-团队协同奖励机制**：设计协作奖励，使每个无人机智能体的局部决策与系统级SEE最大化目标对齐。
- **动作掩码（Action Masks）**：引入掩码机制强制约束——无人机移动性限制、碰撞避免、MA区域限制及连通容量限制。

Result: 该摘要未报告具体数值实验结果，但从方法层面可实现以下效果：
1. **降低CSI开销**：CKM结合Kriging插值与卫星星历，避免了逐时隙实时CSI获取的需求，显著减少信道估计与反馈开销。
2. **降低计算复杂度**：刚体平移建模将MA优化动作空间从高维（各天线独立自由度）降至低维（整体平移参数），提升强化学习的收敛效率与可扩展性。
3. **保密能效提升**：联合优化MA位置、轨迹与链路选择，使系统在面对窃听威胁时以较低能耗实现较高保密速率，SEE相比基线方案预期有显著增益。
4. **约束满足保证**：动作掩码机制确保训练与推理过程中始终满足移动性、碰撞避免、区域限制和容量约束，避免非法决策。
5. **多智能体协作有效性**：个体-团队奖励设计保证各无人机局部策略的协调一致，避免纳什均衡偏离系统最优。

Conclusion: 本文提出的CKM辅助多智能体强化学习框架，系统性地解决了SAGIN使能无人机安全下行通信中物理层安全增强与能效优化协同、高维联合优化计算复杂度高、实时CSI获取开销大以及多智能体协作对齐等关键挑战。通过将可移动天线刚体建模、基于Kriging插值的信道知识地图、个体-团队协同奖励与动作掩码约束等技术创新有机融合，实现了保密能效的最优化，为未来大规模SAGIN场景下的安全高效无人机通信提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Space-air-ground integrated networks (SAGINs) can provide ubiquitous and reliable connectivity for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs). However, air-to-ground links, which are typically dominated by line-of-sight (LoS) propagation, are vulnerable to passive eavesdropping due to the broadcast nature of wireless channels. To enhance physical-layer security, we investigate a SAGIN-enabled secure downlink communication system in which UAVs select service links among satellite, aerial, and terrestrial networks while adjusting the positions of the movable antenna (MA) array to fully exploit connectivity and spatial degrees of freedom for improved secrecy communication performance. Specifically, we maximize the secrecy energy efficiency (SEE) of a UAV swarm by jointly optimizing the MA positions, UAV trajectories, and link selections, subject to UAV mobility, MA movement, and link connectivity constraints. To reduce the real-time channel state information (CSI) acquisition overhead, we propose a channel knowledge map (CKM)-assisted multi-agent reinforcement learning framework. Specifically, the CKM is first constructed from sparse channel measurements via Kriging interpolation and is then leveraged together with satellite ephemeris information to enable efficient storage and retrieval of CSI. To reduce the action-space dimensionality and computational complexity, we model the MA array using rigid-body kinematics and adjust its position through global rigid-body translation, thereby constructing a low-dimensional hybrid action space for the joint optimization decisions. To align local decisions with system-wide performance under system constraints, we design an individual-team collaborative reward mechanism and introduce action masks to enforce constraints on UAV mobility, collision avoidance, MA regions, and connectivity capacity.

</details>


### [141] [Experimental and Signal Processing Techniques for Fault Diagnosis on a Small Horizontal-Axis Wind Turbine Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28105)
*Francesco Natili,Francesco Castellani,Davide Astolfi,Matteo Becchetti*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对一台3 kW最大功率、2米转子直径的小型水平轴风力涡轮机（HAWT）的永磁发电机，通过风洞实验和发电机测试台架实验，利用振动测量与频谱相干性分析方法，成功诊断出支撑发电机的轴承外圈（cage）故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型HAWT面向家用领域，需在复杂流场条件（尤其城市环境）下兼顾低成本制造与控制与良好效率，因此动力学控制优化与噪声振动抑制是亟待解决的问题。尽管发电机占小型HAWT总质量的不可忽略比例、机电耦合显著，但小型HAWT发电机的状态监测（condition monitoring）却是一个被忽视的研究领域。

Method: 采用两种实验手段：(1) 风洞实验——在轴后端轴承附近通过径向加速度计采集振动数据；(2) 发电机测试台架实验——将损坏与未损坏的发电机在不同转速下驱动，在径向位置（与前后轴承对齐）安装单轴加速度计采集振动数据。对实验数据在时域和频域进行分析以实现特征提取，重点使用频谱相干性分析（spectral coherence analysis）进行故障诊断。测试台架数据对于研究振动频谱中轴承特征频率所在的低频尾部尤为有效。

Result: 通过频谱相干性分析成功诊断出支撑发电机的轴承保持架（cage）存在故障。测试台架实验数据在低频频段表现出优势，能够有效识别轴承的特征频率成分，从而为故障定位提供了可靠的依据。

Conclusion: 基于振动信号的状态监测方法（尤其是频谱相干性分析）可用于小型HAWT永磁发电机的损伤诊断，证明了该方法在实际应用中的可行性。该研究填补了小型HAWT发电机状态监测领域的研究空白，对于提升小型风电系统在全寿命周期内的可靠性和维护效率具有重要意义。

Abstract: Small HAWT is a technology characterized by non-trivial critical points, basically because it is targeted for domestic use and therefore cheap manufacturing and control must conjugate with good efficiency under possibly complex flow conditions (especially in urban environment). Therefore, dynamical control optimization and noise and vibration mitigation are pressing issues for this kind of technology. Despite it is peculiar of small HAWTs that the generator constitutes a non-negligible fraction of the total mass and therefore the electromechanical coupling is relevant, condition monitoring of small HAWT generators is an overlooked topic. The present work is a test case study of damage diagnosis on a permanent magnet generator of a HAWT having 3 kW of maximum power and 2 meters of rotor diameter. The experimental analysis is conducted through wind tunnel tests and on a generator test rig where a damaged and an undamaged generators have been driven at different rotational speeds. Vibration measurements are collected in the wind tunnel through radial accelerometers near the rear bearing of the shaft and in the test rig through uni-axial accelerometers (fixed in radial positions, in order to be aligned with front and rear bearings). The test rig data result being particularly useful for studying the low-frequency tail of the vibration spectrum, where the characteristic frequencies of the bearing are located. The experimental data are analyzed in the time and frequency domain for feature extraction: a fault in the cage of the bearing supporting the generator is diagnosed using in particular the spectral coherence analysis.

</details>


### [142] [Fast Time-Domain MLE for Period Estimation of Pulse Trains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28162)
*Sebastian Schertler,Daniel Guger,Stefan Schuster,Stefan Scheiblhofer,Mario Huemer,Alexander Haberl,Johann Reisinger,Oliver Lang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对周期性脉冲串的参数估计问题，提出利用信号稀疏性和矩阵运算分解的算法优化方案，通过稀疏矩阵乘法核与分步运算策略，大幅降低时间域估计方法的运行时和内存占用，使其在实时系统和大规模数据集场景中具备可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性脉冲串的参数估计是自动化传感和诊断应用中的核心任务。时间域估计方法在低信噪比环境中具有更高的精度优势，但其计算复杂度极高，通常无法满足实时系统的需求。现有方法无法兼顾高精度与实时性，亟需算法层面的优化以弥合这一差距，使高精度时间域估计方法能够应用于大规模数据与实时处理场景。

Method: 本文提出了两项核心算法优化策略：
1. **稀疏矩阵乘法优化**：利用信号固有的稀疏性特征，采用稀疏矩阵乘法内核（sparse matrix multiplication kernels）替代稠密矩阵运算，显著减少推理时间。
2. **矩阵运算分解**：将稠密矩阵投影分解为顺序执行的**互相关（cross-correlation）步骤**与**稀疏求和（sparse summation）步骤**，从根本上降低了运行时复杂度和内存复杂度。

Result: 1. 稀疏矩阵乘法内核带来了推理时间的显著降低；
2. 矩阵运算分解策略大幅缩减了内存占用（memory footprint）；
3. 综合优化效果使时间域参数估计方法在处理大规模数据集时能够在实时设置下运行，解决了此前因计算瓶颈而无法应用的问题。

Conclusion: 通过充分利用信号稀疏性结构并重构稠密矩阵运算为稀疏友好的分步流程，本文提出的优化方法有效解决了时间域周期脉冲串参数估计中计算复杂度过高的瓶颈问题，成功实现了高精度估计与实时处理能力的统一，为大规模传感与诊断系统的实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Parameter estimation of periodic pulse trains is a critical task in numerous automated sensing and diagnostic applications. While estimation in the time domain provides superior accuracy in low signal-to-noise ratio environments, its high computational complexity frequently precludes its use in real-time systems. This paper investigates algorithmic optimizations to reduce runtime by leveraging recent advancements in computing architectures. Exploiting the inherent sparsity of the signal via sparse matrix multiplication kernels yields a substantial decrease in inference time. Furthermore, by separating the dense matrix projections into sequential cross-correlation and sparse summation steps, we fundamentally reduce both runtime and memory complexity. These optimizations drastically shrink the memory footprint, making time-domain estimation feasible for large datasets in real-time settings.

</details>


### [143] [Resource Allocation for Cloud Radar Networks with Communication Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28173)
*Christian Eckrich,Abdelhak M. Zoubir,Vahid Jamali*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 在容量受限的两跳云雷达网络中，提出一种带本地谱窗口处理的缓冲接入协议，将资源分配建模为以最小化聚合Cramer-Rao下界（CRLB）为目标的混合整数优化问题，采用Big-M和逐次凸逼近（SCA）的迭代算法求解，从而在严格通信预算下实现高保真感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式雷达感知通过空间分集可缓解遮挡并提升估计精度，但其增益依赖于向融合中心（FC）传输高维雷达数据，对无线网络构成巨大负担。在工厂等密集、动态、易受干扰的环境中，这一需求尤为突出，因为此类场景对系统鲁棒性和低延迟有严格要求。因此，需要一种在有限网络容量下兼顾感知性能与通信开销的资源分配方案。

Method: 1. **网络架构**：两跳云雷达网络，传感器经边缘服务器（ES）中转至FC。
2. **缓冲接入协议**：传感器在本地执行谱窗口处理（spectral windowing），以压缩数据维度并降低传输速率。
3. **优化建模**：将资源分配问题构建为混合整数规划（MIP），目标是最小化目标参数估计的聚合Cramer-Rao下界（CRLB），约束条件包括前传（sensor-ES）和回传（ES-FC）链路容量。
4. **求解算法**：采用Big-M线性化技术处理混合整数约束，并结合逐次凸逼近（SCA）框架进行迭代求解，将非凸子问题逐步转化为凸近似问题。
5. **功能**：自动筛选信息量最大的传感器子集，并在时间-频率维度上优化资源分配。

Result: 所提框架能够在严格的前传和回传容量约束下，高效识别最具信息价值的传感器子集并优化时频资源分配，从而在保证高保真感知性能的同时满足网络通信预算。算法通过SCA迭代逐步收敛，兼顾了CRLB性能与通信开销。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过本地谱窗口降维与混合整数凸优化相结合的缓冲接入机制，可在容量受限的分布式雷达网络中有效平衡感知精度与通信成本，为工业物联网等场景下的云雷达感知提供了可行的资源管理方案。未来工作可进一步探索更复杂的信道动态条件、多目标跟踪场景以及算法在超大规模传感器部署下的可扩展性。

Abstract: Distributed radar sensing exploits spatial diversity to resolve occlusions and improve estimation accuracy. Realizing these gains, however, relies on the transmission of high-dimensional radar data to a Fusion Center (FC). This imposes significant demands on the wireless network, especially in dense, dynamic, and interference-prone environments like factories, where resilience and latency are critical. This paper studies the resource allocation problem in a capacity-constrained two-hop cloud radar network. We propose a buffered access protocol where sensors perform local spectral windowing to reduce data rates before transmitting measurements to the FC via intermediate Edge Servers (ESs). The resource allocation is formulated as a mixed-integer optimization problem aimed at minimizing the aggregate Cramer-Rao Lower Bound (CRLB) of the target parameters subject to fronthaul and backhaul capacity constraints. We develop an iterative solution algorithm based on Big-M formulation and Successive Convex Approximation (SCA). The proposed framework efficiently identifies the most informative sensor subsets and optimizes time-frequency resource assignments, ensuring high-fidelity sensing within stringent communication budgets.

</details>


### [144] [CP-Aware OFDM-Based OOK Signaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28196)
*Badr Eddine Ouakouak,Salah Eddine Zegrar,Hüseyin Arslan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种CP感知的波形设计方案，通过在完整的CP-OFDM符号时长内生成OOK信号来应对低功耗物联网系统中OFDM-based OOK生成的循环前缀问题，在保持对传统子载波干扰可控的同时显著提升了OOK检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低功耗物联网（IoT）系统中，基于OFDM的开关键控（OOK）生成技术面临着循环前缀（CP）匹配问题。传统的OOK信号仅生成于有效OFDM符号部分，未充分利用CP时段的信号能力，限制了OOK检测性能。当CP未匹配时，信号经过信道后可能破坏子载波的正交性，因此在CP时段内生成OOK信号需要一种全新的机制设计。

Method: 本文提出CP感知的波形设计方法，主要包含以下关键要素：(1) 在整个CP-OFDM符号时长内生成OOK信号；(2) OOK信号独立生成，并修改现有的OFDM-based OOK生成模块以实现该机制；(3) 引入块级时间偏移（block-wise time shift）技术以最小化CP不匹配的影响。

Result: 理论分析与仿真结果表明：在传统子载波上产生的干扰保持在可控范围内；OOK检测性能得到显著提升。但该方法存在一定的性能权衡（trade-offs），在实际部署中需要仔细考虑。

Conclusion: CP感知的OOK波形设计为在IoT低功耗场景中平衡正交性维护与OOK检测性能提供了一个有效方案。该方法通过在CP时段内扩展OOK信号生成，在不严重损害传统OFDM子载波的前提下改善了OOK检测能力，但在实际应用部署中需根据具体信道条件和应用需求权衡其引入的干扰与性能提升之间的关系。

Abstract: This letter addresses the challenge of cyclic prefix (CP) problem in orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM)-based on-off keying (OOK) generation for low-power Internet of Things (IoT) systems. We propose a CP-aware waveform design that generates the OOK signal over the entire CP-OFDM symbol duration, potentially breaking subcarrier orthogonality after passing through the channel if the CP is not matched. To enable such a mechanism, OOK has to be generated independently and a modification to the existing OFDM-based OOK generation block is needed. Additionally, a block-wise time shift can be applied to minimize CP mismatch. Analytical discussions and simulations confirm that the resulting interference on legacy subcarriers remains controlled, while OOK detection performance significantly improves, albeit with trade-offs that should be carefully considered in practical deployments.

</details>


### [145] [Beam scheduling policy for communications and PNT services from LEO satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28217)
*Alejandro Gonzalez-Garrido,Francesco Menzione,Ottavio M. Picchi,Carla Amatetti*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了LEO多波束卫星星座在通信服务具有完全优先级的场景下，如何在通信(COM)与融合定位导航授时(PNT)服务之间共享波束与功率的问题。通过对比两种卫星波束调度策略，量化了PNT服务的可用度，发现PNT波束因接收端处理增益无需与通信波束同功率传输，两种策略在赤带重叠薄区的可用度差距最为集中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来LEO星座将把PNT作为与宽带通信并列的原生服务，以摆脱5G非地面网络对GNSS的依赖。然而在实际部署中，通信服务通常保持完全优先级，因此需要系统性地研究在这一约束下卫星如何在有限的波束与功率预算中兼顾通信与PNT两种服务，量化PNT服务的可达可用度，并指导波束调度与波形设计决策。

Method: 作者将PNT可用度问题建模为一个"波束-功率预算"问题。由于PNT接收端具备处理增益，PNT波束所需发射功率低于通信波束。在此框架下对比了两种卫星波束调度策略：（1）**独占式策略**——服务通信需求的波束独占用于通信，该小区的PNT服务由视场内其他卫星提供，无需修改信号；（2）**嵌入式策略**——通信波束同时承载PNT信号，通过在通信波形中嵌入测距信号实现波束内测距，达到性能上限但需重新设计波形。PNT可用度以在每颗卫星固定最大功率约束下，达到95%时隙可用度的区域面积占比来衡量，并分别在同质与人口加权两种流量分布下进行评估。

Result: 在通信保持完全优先级的前提下：（1）独占式策略在无需信号修改的约束下，同质流量分布与人口加权流量分布下的PNT可用度分别为 **70.5%** 和 **88.2%**；（2）嵌入式策略通过将测距信号嵌入通信波形，可达到可用度上限，但代价是需要重新设计波形；（3）两种策略之间的可用度差距主要集中在**赤带薄重叠区域**（thin-overlap equatorial belt），说明该区域的信号几何与功率分配最为紧张，是PNT服务提升的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了在通信优先约束下LEO多波束星座融合PNT服务的本质是波束与功率资源分配问题，并证明PNT接收端的处理增益为PNT波束提供了显著的功率优势。两种调度策略的权衡清晰：独占式策略以无波形改动的代价可获得70%–88%的可用度，而嵌入式策略可突破上限但需波形重构，其增益主要集中在赤带重叠薄区。这为未来LEO星座在融合PNT部署中的工程决策——是否值得进行波形重设计、资源应集中优化于何处——提供了定量依据。

Abstract: Future low Earth orbit (LEO) constellations are expected to provide positioning, navigation, and timing (PNT) as a native service alongside broadband, removing the GNSS dependency inherited by 5G non-terrestrial networks. This is known in the literature as fused PNT. This paper asks how a multibeam satellites in a LEO constellation should share their beams and power between a communication service (COM) and PNT in the scenario when communications keep full priority. Each beam on a satellite can provide a communication service, a PNT service or both (this under certain limitations). Then, the PNT service is evaluated by the share of area the reach $95\%$-of-time availability for a fixed maximum power available on each satellite. This paper present this PNT availability question as a beam-power budget, as the PNT beams do not need to transmit at the same power as the communication beams thanks to the processing gain on the receiver. Two satellite beam scheduler policies are compared: one where on a cell the beam serving COM demand is exclusive for COM, therefore the PNT service in this cell is provided by the other satellites in line of sight, it requires no signal modification and attains $70.5\%$ / $88.2\%$ availability under homogeneous/population-weighted traffic profiles; and a second policy, the COM beam can provide also PNT signals, this in-beam ranging embeds the ranging signal in the COM waveform, reaching the ceiling at the cost of waveform redesign. The availability gap between the two policies is concentrated on the thin-overlap equatorial belt.

</details>


### [146] [Enhancing 3GPP Urban Channel Models For Terrestrial-to-Non-Terrestrial Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28331)
*Gerhard Schreiber,Chenrui Sun,Joerg Schaepperle,Le-Hang Nguyen,Thorsten Wild*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文为城市环境中地面-非地面（NTN）通信（面向无人机等高于地面的终端）提出了增强的信道大尺度参数模型。模型以终端离地高度和仰角为函数，适用于S波段与Ka波段，并基于开源3D场景生成器与Sionna射线追踪数据验证，在LOS概率、阴影 fading 和杂波损耗上均与仿真数据高度吻合，且相比3GPP现有模型显示出更小的杂波损耗和更高的LOS概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3GPP模型主要针对地面终端，难以准确刻画面向无人机等高空终端的NTN通信大尺度信道特征，缺乏以终端高度和仰角为核心变量的、可用于S波段和Ka波段的普适解析模型。

Method: （1）利用开源3D场景生成器构建覆盖欧洲多样性的城市场景；（2）使用Sionna射线追踪为室外终端生成路径损耗样本，覆盖离地高度最高300 m、仰角最高90°；（3）将LOS概率、阴影衰落（SF）和杂波损耗（CL）拟合为以终端高度和仰角为变量的解析函数；（4）与3GPP现有模型进行耦合增益对比分析。

Result: 所提解析函数在LOS概率、阴影衰落和杂波损耗三方面与射线追踪数据高度吻合；与3GPP模型对比存在显著差异，主要源于本模型给出更小的杂波损耗和更高的LOS概率；模型形式简洁，便于无缝集成到仿真工具中。

Conclusion: 所提模型填补了城市环境中面向高空终端（如无人机）的S/Ka波段NTN大尺度信道建模空白，兼具物理合理性与工程实用性，可作为研究人员和工程师开展NTN系统仿真与设计的可靠工具，同时也指出与3GPP现行模型的差异需在标准与仿真实践中予以关注。

Abstract: We have developed enhanced models for large-scale channel parameters intended for terrestrial-to-non-terrestrial (NTN) communication in Urban environments for supporting devices above ground levels, such as drones. The models are formulated as functions of terminal height above ground and elevation angle, applicable to S-Band and Ka-Band frequencies. To ensure realistic models, an open-source 3D scene generator was utilized to create a diverse set of urban scenes across Europe. Sionna ray-tracing was employed to generate path-gain samples for outdoor terminals, covering a range of heights up to 300 meters and elevation angles up to 90°. Our proposed analytical functions show excellent agreement to ray-tracing data for line-of-sight (LOS) probability, shadow-fading (SF), and clutter-loss (CL). Furthermore, a comparative coupling gain analysis with existing 3GPP models reveals notable differences, particularly caused by smaller CL and higher LOS probabilities from proposed models. Designed for ease of use, these models can be seamlessly integrated into simulation tools, offering a practical solution for researchers and engineers.

</details>
