Alarm-gated mid-execution oversight recovers 23 of 70 failures while spending a frontier overseer on 38, giving a deployable cascade at mean score 86.8 and 84.5% full-solve (305 of 361). The certificate bounds false alarms only. We also report where behavioral monitoring is uninformative.

</details>


### [203] [When Robots Mishear Us: Mapping the Safety Risks of Voice-Controlled Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28518)
*Sihan Jia,Oliver Lemon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了自动语音识别（ASR）错误是否会损害具身AI（EAI）模型的安全性。研究发现，ASR识别错误可能导致有害指令被具身AI模型接受并执行，从而显著降低系统安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语音交互成为具身AI的主要输入方式之一，ASR系统在自然语言处理流程中的准确性和鲁棒性变得至关重要。然而，现有的具身AI安全研究主要关注文本层面的安全边界，忽视了语音识别环节引入的错误对下游具身AI模型安全性的潜在影响。本文旨在填补这一研究空白，系统性地评估ASR错误对具身AI安全性的实际威胁。

Method: 研究通过模拟ASR错误，并将其与现有的具身AI安全基准测试（SafeAgentBench和POEX）相结合，评估不同类型的ASR错误对具身AI安全性的影响。具体方法包括：(1)构建模拟ASR错误场景；(2)利用SafeAgentBench和POEX等安全基准测试框架进行系统性评估；(3)分析不同ASR错误类型对语义结构保持和模型拒绝行为的影响；(4)验证自动纠错方法在降低安全风险方面的有效性。

Result: 1. 部分ASR错误虽保留了语义结构，但增加了有害歧义，使原本无害的指令被误理解为有害指令；2. 另一些ASR错误会削弱模型的安全拒绝行为，导致不安全计划的生成与执行；3. ASR错误的自动纠正虽在某些场景下能降低风险，但并非总是有效；4. 总体而言，ASR错误对具身AI造成了显著的安全风险。

Conclusion: 自动语音识别（ASR）错误对具身AI系统构成了不可忽视的安全威胁。ASR层面的微小识别偏差可能在具身AI的决策执行层被放大，导致有害指令被执行。这表明在多模态具身AI系统中，语音识别的鲁棒性是安全架构中不可或缺的一环。未来的具身AI安全设计需要将ASR环节的潜在错误纳入整体安全评估框架，并在语音理解与指令执行之间建立更有效的安全防护机制。

Abstract: We investigate whether automatic speech recognition (ASR) errors in user input can lead to unsafe outputs from Embodied AI (EAI) models. We find that ASR errors can lead to harmful instructions being accepted and executed by EAI models, thereby reducing safety. We simulate ASR errors and combine them with existing safety benchmarks (SafeAgentBench and POEX) to evaluate how different errors affect embodied AI safety. We find that some of them preserve semantic structure but increase harmful ambiguity, while others weaken the model refusal behaviour and allow unsafe plans to be generated and executed. We show that in some cases automatic correction of ASR errors can reduce the risk, but this is not always effective. Overall, we show that ASR errors lead to significant safety risks for embodied AI.

</details>


### [204] [AcCoRD: Evaluating User-Agent Collaboration Under Realistic User Preference Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27818)
*Tejas Srinivasan,Shikib Mehri,Nandita Shankar Naik,Anirban Das,William M. Campbell,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AcCoRD基准，一个评估智能体处理用户偏好动态变化的协作能力，揭示前沿LLM在应对偏好演化与不确定性方面能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的用户偏好并非静态且一次性给出，而是在交互过程中逐步形成、揭示、调整和放松。现有用户-智能体协作基准几乎只关注解决"信息不足"（underspecified）的偏好问题，无法捕捉真实交互中更丰富的动态特性。作者旨在构建一个能够全面覆盖偏好动态演化的评测基准。

Method: 1. **数据集构建**：设计AcCoRD基准，涵盖在线购物和旅行规划两个领域，要求智能体处理多样的用户偏好动态（偏好出现、变化、放松等）。
2. **评测模型**：选取五个前沿大语言模型（frontier LLMs）进行评估。
3. **提示策略**：采用两种提示方式进行对比——(a) 标准的vanilla ReAct策略；(b) 不确定性引导变体（uncertainty-guided），提示模型主动识别和解决用户偏好中的歧义。

Result: 1. 前沿模型能够处理信息不足（underspecification）的偏好场景。
2. 但在偏好于交互中途产生或演化的场景中表现不佳，说明需要更复杂的不确定性建模能力。
3. 仅靠提示（prompting）无法有效激发模型对不确定性的识别能力。

Conclusion: 真实用户偏好具有动态演化的特性，当前前沿LLM在协作交互中缺乏对此动态的充分应对能力。提示工程不足以弥补这一能力缺口，需要更根本的模型改进。AcCoRD被发布为开发能够应对完整偏好动态的协作智能体的评测资源。

Abstract: User preferences in user-agent collaboration are rarely static and fully-specified upfront: preferences are formed, revealed, adjusted, and relaxed during interaction. Existing benchmarks for evaluating user-agent collaboration focus almost exclusively on resolving underspecified preferences, thereby failing to capture the richer dynamics of real-world interaction. We introduce AcCoRD, a user-agent collaboration benchmark requiring agents to handle diverse user preference dynamics in two domains: online shopping and travel planning. We evaluate five frontier LLMs under two prompting strategies: vanilla ReAct, and an uncertainty-guided variant that prompts models to identify and resolve ambiguity about user preferences. Our results reveal that frontier models can handle underspecification but struggle to satisfy preferences that emerge or evolve mid-interaction and require more sophisticated uncertainty modeling. Further, prompting alone fails to elicit the required uncertainty recognition. We release AcCoRD as a resource for developing agents that can navigate the full complexity of real-world user preferences.

</details>


### [205] [Evidential-Based Higher-Order Set Argumentation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27824)
*Shuai Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了证据型高阶集合论证框架（EHSAF），统一了多种现有形式体系，并开发了两种完整语义（相邻完整标记语义与基于扩展的完整语义），同时给出了基于三值Łukasiewicz逻辑和连续模糊逻辑（Gödel、Product、Łukasiewicz）的正常命题编码，实现了定性证据推理与定量证据推理之间的桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的证据型论证形式体系存在三个关键缺陷：(1) 缺乏对证据支持（evidential support）的统一处理；(2) 未统一处理高阶关系（即攻击和支持可以指向任意元素，而非仅限于论证）；(3) 未能将交互的来源表示为集合（集合交互）。因此，需要一种能够在单一表达性设置下保守地推广多个现有框架的统一框架，并为该框架建立完整的语义与计算推理能力。

Method: 1. 提出EHSAF框架：在Dung抽象论证的基础上扩展，要求论证及其交互由基于prima-facie元素的证据链支撑，同时统一处理证据支持、高阶关系和集合交互。
2. 开发两种完整语义：(a) 相邻完整标记语义（adjacent complete labelling semantics）——允许论证取三值（true/false/undecided），在支持循环中对未来证据持开放的认识论态度；(b) 基于扩展的完整语义（extension-based complete semantics）——采取严格的证据主义立场，仅接受具有良好基础支持链的论证。
3. 分析两种语义的关系：证明在支持循环存在时二者发散，在支持无环（support-acyclic）条件下二者等价。
4. 命题编码：给出EHSAF的正常命题编码，并证明在三值Łukasiewicz逻辑中，其模型恰好对应相邻完整标记。
5. 模糊扩展：将编码推广到连续模糊逻辑（Gödel、Product、Łukasiewicz），定义连续模糊正常编码语义，并证明其满足连续性、单调性、边界条件和解存在性；进一步证明在自然t-norm条件下，其三元化可恢复相邻完整标记。

Result: 1. 成功构建了统一的EHSAF框架，保守地推广了多种现有证据型论证形式体系。
2. 证明了两种完整语义在支持循环下发散、在支持无环下等价，明确了二者的适用范围。
3. 建立了EHSAF与三值Łukasiewicz逻辑模型的精确对应关系，为计算推理提供了理论基础。
4. 成功将编码扩展到三种连续模糊逻辑，证明了模糊语义的关键性质（连续性、单调性、边界条件、解存在性），并证明了三元化可恢复三值标记。
5. 实现了定性论证推理与定量证据推理之间的统一，弥合了二者之间的鸿沟。

Conclusion: EHSAF框架及其配套语义和编码方案为证据型论证提供了一个统一、表达力强且计算上可操作的理论基础。通过引入三值与模糊语义，该工作不仅保持了传统定性论证推理的优势，还引入了连续的定量证据度量化能力，为未来在复杂证据场景（如支持循环、高阶交互和集合来源）中进行更精细的论证推理奠定了基础。

Abstract: Evidential argumentation extends Dung's abstract argumentation by requiring arguments and interactions to be backed by chains of evidence rooted in prima-facie elements. However, existing formalisms lack a unified treatment of evidential support, higher-order relations (attacks and supports targeting arbitrary elements), and collective interactions (sources as sets). In this paper, we introduce the Evidential-Based Higher-Order Set Argumentation Framework (EHSAF), which conservatively generalises several existing frameworks within a single expressive setting. We develop two complete semantics for EHSAFs: an \emph{adjacent complete labelling semantics} that admits multiple truth values (true, false, undecided) for arguments in support cycles, reflecting an open epistemic attitude toward future evidence; and an \emph{extension-based complete semantics} that follows a strict evidentialist stance, accepting only arguments with well-founded support chains. We show that these two semantics diverge in the presence of support cycles, and prove their equivalence under support-acyclicity. To enable computational reasoning, we provide a normal propositional encoding of EHSAFs and prove that, in three-valued Łukasiewicz logic, its models correspond precisely to the adjacent complete labellings. We further extend this encoding to continuous fuzzy logics (G{ö}del, Product, and Łukasiewicz), defining a continuous fuzzy normal encoded semantics. We establish that this fuzzy semantics satisfies key properties---continuity, monotonicity, boundary conditions, and solution existence---and that its ternarisation recovers the adjacent complete labellings under natural t-norm conditions. Our framework thus unifies expressive argumentation with principled three-valued and fuzzy semantics, bridging the gap between qualitative and quantitative reasoning about evidence.

</details>


### [206] [RealSWE: A Compositional Evaluation of Coding Agents under Realistic User Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27831)
*Gyuhyeong Kim,Hyojung Gwon,Jeonghyeon Kim,Kyuhong Shim,Sunjae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了SWE-bench系列基准任务（长、正式、结构化）与真实用户编程请求（短、随意、信息有限）之间的显著差距。作者构建了一个名为 sys 的多变体任务族基准（381个），系统性地操控信息组成和语言风格，发现仅明确陈述"期望行为"和"动机"即可显著提升LLM的软件工程表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体的评测基准（如SWE-bench）主要源自精选的GitHub Issue，特点是长、结构化且信息丰富。然而，真实用户请求通常更短、更不结构化、更随意。作者量化了这一差距：88%的真实提示仅包含问题陈述和有限上下文（而基准中仅占7%）；87%的真实提示以随意风格书写（而基准中94%为正式风格）。这引发了一个重要问题——当前基准是否真的反映了模型在实际使用中的能力？以及如何弥合这一差距？

Method: 1. **信息分类法**：定义六类信息维度（如问题陈述、期望行为、动机、环境信息、复现步骤等）和四个语言风格维度。
2. **统计分析**：将该分类法应用于SWE-chat中的真实用户提示以及SWE-bench Verified和Pro中的问题陈述，量化分布差异。
3. **基准构建（sys）**：从SWE-bench Verified和Pro中派生381个多变体任务族。每个族内各变体共享相同的底层任务和黄金补丁，仅在信息组成和语言风格上有所不同。
4. **评估**：在sys上评估七个当代LLM，控制信息类别和风格的变化，进行受控分析。

Result: i) **真实输入的影响**：使用更贴近真实场景的输入后，求解率平均下降6.4个百分点，且可能改变模型排名。
ii) **信息类别的贡献**：
   - "期望行为"和"动机"的包含与否对性能有显著影响；
   - "环境信息"和"复现步骤"仅增加token数量，无显著性能收益。
iii) **语言风格的影响**：仅产生轻微的、依赖于模型的效果。

Conclusion: 本文的核心结论是：真实用户提示中通常省略的"期望行为"和"动机"信息，实际上对LLM的软件工程能力有实质性提升作用。这一发现对用户和智能体具有可操作的指导意义——在编写编程请求时，明确说明期望行为和动机是最具成本效益的方式。同时，本文也指出当前评测基准过于形式化和信息冗余，未能准确反映真实使用场景，呼吁基准设计更加贴近实际。

Abstract: Coding agents are now commonly evaluated on the SWE-bench family of benchmarks, whose tasks are built from curated GitHub issues--long, structured, and information-rich. Real user requests, however, are typically far shorter and less structured. To characterize this gap, we define a six-category information taxonomy and four dimensions of linguistic style, and apply them to real user prompts from SWE-chat and problem statements from SWE-bench Verified and Pro. We find that requests carrying only a problem statement, alone or with limited additional context, account for 88% of real prompts but just 7% of benchmark problems. Furthermore, 87% of real prompts are casually written whereas 94% of benchmark problems are formal. Guided by these observations, we introduce sys, 381 multi-variant task families derived from SWE-bench Verified and Pro. Variants within each family share the same underlying task and gold patch while differing only in information composition and linguistic style. Evaluating seven contemporary LLMs with sys, we find that i) realistic inputs reduce resolution rates by 6.4 pp on average and can change model rankings. Controlled analysis further shows that ii) including Desired Behavior and Motivation significantly affects performance, whereas Environment Information and Reproduction Steps merely add tokens without measurable benefit; iii) linguistic style has only small, model-dependent effects. These findings provide actionable guidance for users and agents: explicitly stating the desired behavior and motivation--which most real prompts omit--substantially improves the LLM's software engineering performance.

</details>


### [207] [KLOD: Locality-Preserving Knowledge Editing via Non-Target Distribution Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27839)
*Hojun Jeong,Gyunyeop Kim,Sangwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KLOD（一种有界且保持分布的知识编辑目标函数），用于微调式知识编辑，通过将目标更新与需要保持稳定的分布分离，有效缓解序列编辑中的局域性退化问题，同时在CounterFact和ZsRE数据集上保持高编辑可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调式知识编辑虽然简洁且具有架构无关性，但标准交叉熵损失仅提升被编辑目标token的概率，并未显式约束非目标输出分布的变化。在序列编辑场景下，这种无约束的概率重新分配会累积为分布漂移（distributional drift），从而导致局域性退化（locality degradation）。

Method: KLOD通过以下方式工作：1）分离意图的目标更新与应保持稳定的分布；2）对目标概率设阈值，一旦达到阈值即停止放大，避免过度编辑；3）在目标位置保留去除目标后的非目标分布（target-excluded non-target distribution）；4）在prefix位置保留完整的next-token分布。该目标函数具有有界性（bounded）和分布保持性（distribution-preserving），实现了对编辑效果的可控调节。

Result: 在CounterFact和ZsRE数据集上，使用Llama3-8B-Instruct和Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型进行实验：KLOD在保持高编辑可靠性的同时显著缓解了局域性退化。目标概率阈值提供了可控制的泛化-局域性权衡（Generalization-Locality trade-off）。消融实验、多种子实验和分布KL分析均支持一个结论：KLOD的局域性提升源于保持输出分布，而非简单地削弱编辑效果。

Conclusion: KLOD提出了一种有效的有界且分布保持的知识编辑目标函数，核心洞察是将目标更新与分布保持解耦。通过在目标位置约束非目标分布、在prefix位置约束完整分布，并引入概率阈值停止机制，KLOD在不牺牲编辑可靠性的前提下显著改善了序列编辑的局域性。消融实验和KL分析证实局域性增益确实来自分布保持而非编辑强度弱化，证明该方法设计原理的合理性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Fine-tuning-based knowledge editing is simple and architecture-agnostic, but standard cross-entropy increases the edited target probability without explicitly constraining changes in the non-target output distribution. In sequential editing, such unconstrained redistribution can accumulate as distributional drift and contribute to locality degradation. We propose KLOD, a bounded and distribution-preserving objective for fine-tuning-based knowledge editing that separates the intended target update from distributions that should remain stable. KLOD stops target amplification once a probability threshold is reached, while preserving the target-excluded non-target distribution at target positions and the full next-token distribution at prefix positions. Experiments on CounterFact and ZsRE with Llama3-8B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct show that KLOD substantially mitigates locality degradation while maintaining high edit reliability. The target probability threshold further provides a controllable Generalization--Locality trade-off. Ablation, multi-seed, and distributional KL analyses support the interpretation that KLOD's locality gains are associated with preserving output distributions rather than simply weakening the edit. Code is available on GitHub https://github.com/Hostoday/KLOD .

</details>


### [208] [An Empirical Evaluation of Cross-City POI Recommendation on a Large-Scale Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27840)
*Peibo Li,Yang Song,Hao Xue,Maarten de Rijke,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用大规模基准 Trip World 重新检验跨城市 POI 推荐方法的结论，发现小数据集上的结论在全世界覆盖、低区域重叠和语义丰富的 POI 清单条件下并不成立。评测揭示了三类瓶颈： hometown-aware 模型依赖目的地区域先验而非用户偏好迁移、规模扩大后精度-效率权衡恶化（最简单模型反而最强）、语义元数据整合机制收益甚微。此外，从下一站 POI 推荐移植的 agentic 方法表现甚至不及简单的流行度先验。结果表明亟需面向跨城偏好迁移、语义锚定和可扩展推理的专用设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨城市 POI 推荐对于导航陌生城市至关重要，但历史进展受数据规模限制。此前研究多基于小规模基准，其结论在全世界级别数据、低来源-目的地重叠、大规模语义丰富 POI 清单条件下是否仍然成立尚不明确。作者希望利用新提出大规模基准 Trip World 对代表性 SOTA 方法进行实证复评，揭示规模变化下方法的真实表现与瓶颈。

Method: 1. 采用大规模基准 Trip World 对代表性 SOTA 跨城市 POI 推荐方法进行统一实证评测；
2. 设置条件：全球覆盖、低来源-目的地区域重叠、大规模语义丰富 POI 清单；
3. 对 hometown-aware 模型、简单流行度基线、语义元数据整合方法进行全面对比；
4. 诊断性实验：将从下一站 POI 推荐适配而来的 agentic 方法进行跨城任务实验，考察朴素适配的有效性。

Result: 三类主要发现：(1) hometown-aware 模型实际依赖目的地区域先验（destination-region priors），而非用户级偏好迁移，即模型"看似"利用家乡信息，实则仅利用了地理区域先验；(2) 在大规模场景下精度-效率权衡显著恶化，最简单的模型（流行度先验）反而位于第一梯队；(3) 现有语义元数据整合机制带来的收益极为有限。此外，从下一站 POI 推荐移植的 agentic 方法，尽管数据中存在相关语义信号，其表现仍不及简单流行度先验。

Conclusion: 小规模基准上的结论在全世界规模数据下不可靠。跨城 POI 推荐领域亟需面向任务的设计，应聚焦三方面：(1) 支持真正的跨城市偏好迁移机制，而非依赖目的地区域先验；(2) 有效的语义锚定（semantic grounding）以充分利用 POI 元数据；(3) 在未见过的目的地清单上具备可扩展的推理能力。简单的流行度基线在当前 SOTA 方法面前的竞争力凸显了该领域的显著改进空间。

Abstract: Cross-city point-of-interest (POI) recommendation is crucial for navigating unfamiliar urban environments, yet its progress has historically been constrained by data limitations. Using the recently proposed large-scale benchmark Trip World, we empirically re-examine whether conclusions drawn on small prior benchmarks still hold under worldwide coverage, low home-destination region overlap, and large, semantically rich POI inventories. Our evaluation surfaces three bottlenecks of representative state-of-the-art methods: (1) hometown-aware models appear to rely more on destination-region priors than on user-specific preference transfer; (2) their accuracy-efficiency trade-off degrades at this scale, where the simplest model is among the strongest; and (3) existing mechanisms for integrating semantic metadata yield little benefit. We further include a diagnostic pilot on agentic methods adapted from next-POI recommendation, finding that naive adaptation trails a simple popularity prior even though the relevant semantic signal is present in the data. These results highlight the need for task-specific designs that support cross-city preference transfer, semantic grounding, and scalable reasoning over unseen destination inventories.

</details>


### [209] [From Uncertainty to Clinical Risk: Severity-Aware Conformal Planning for Interactive Medical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27847)
*Yue Zhou,Haiyang Zhou,Jin Zhang,Kong Wang,Yongxin Ni,Youhua Li,Hanwen Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"严重性感知保形临床规划"(Severity-Aware Conformal Clinical Planning, SACP)，将交互式医疗诊断建模为风险敏感的顺序决策问题。该方法利用临床风险（而非仅预测不确定性）作为规划信号，通过保形校准与蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)联合优化问诊与诊断决策，在DDXPlus和MediQ上实现更少问诊轮次下更准确诊断，并显著降低重症漏诊风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 现有交互式诊断方法主要依赖预测不确定性(predictive uncertainty)或标签歧义度(label ambiguity)来引导信息获取，忽视了漏诊重症疾病的非对称临床风险。
2. 缺少统一的长程规划机制来决策"继续追问"还是"直接给出诊断"，即缺乏Ask与Commit两种动作的联合优化。
3. 临床场景中不同疾病严重性不同，漏诊重症的后果远重于误诊轻症，需要风险感知的规划信号。

Method: SACP框架包含三个核心组件：
1. **互补信念维护**：同时维护诊断信念(diagnostic belief)、安全性信念(safety belief)和掩蔽证据信念(masked-evidence belief)三者，以支持临床推理与风险感知。
2. **保形校准**：在留出集诊断轨迹上，对每个问诊轮次(turn-specific)的保形预测集进行校准，并对严重性加权(severity-weighted)的差异诊断风险(differential-diagnosis risk)进行校准。
3. **MCTS长程规划**：将校准后的临床风险引入蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)，用于联合评估"追问(Ask)"与"诊断(Commit)"两种动作的长程轨迹，从而实现风险敏感的顺序决策。

Result: 1. 在DDXPlus和MediQ两个基准上，跨多个大语言模型(LLM)均实现更少问诊次数下更准确的诊断。
2. 差异诊断质量(differential-diagnosis quality)得到提升。
3. 重症案例中的高风险错误(high-risk errors)显著减少，尤其是重症漏诊风险。
4. 验证了以临床风险（而非仅预测不确定性）作为规划信号的价值，证明了该框架在信息获取与风险感知诊断决策方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种新颖的风险敏感交互式诊断框架SACP，通过将严重性加权的临床风险与保形校准结合，并嵌入MCTS进行长程规划，实现了更精准、更高效且更安全的多轮问诊诊断。实验表明该框架在多个LLM和基准上均有效，证实了临床风险作为规划信号的优越性。未来工作可进一步探索以临床风险为导向的交互式诊断与信息获取方法。

Abstract: Interactive medical diagnosis dynamically acquires patient information through multiple rounds of questioning, supporting accurate, efficient, and safe clinical decisions under incomplete evidence. Existing methods commonly guide information acquisition with predictive uncertainty or label ambiguity, but overlook the asymmetric clinical risk of missing severe diseases and lack unified long-horizon planning over whether to continue asking questions or commit to a diagnosis. To address these limitations, we propose Severity-Aware Conformal Clinical Planning, which formulates interactive diagnosis as a risk-sensitive sequential decision problem. The framework maintains complementary diagnostic, safety, and masked-evidence beliefs; calibrates turn-specific diagnostic prediction sets and severity-weighted differential-diagnosis risk on held-out diagnostic trajectories; and introduces the calibrated clinical risk into Monte Carlo Tree Search to jointly evaluate long-horizon Ask and Commit trajectories. Experiments on DDXPlus and MediQ show that our method achieves more accurate diagnoses with fewer questions across multiple large language models, while improving differential-diagnosis quality and reducing high-risk errors in severe cases. These findings validate the value of using clinical risk, rather than predictive uncertainty alone, as a planning signal and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework for information acquisition and risk-aware diagnostic decision making. They also motivate future work on clinical-risk-oriented interactive diagnosis and information-acquisition methods.

</details>


### [210] [SpikeOPD: Stable On-Policy Distillation for Autoregressive Spiking Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27857)
*Enqiao Lu,Xingrui Yu,Yiwei Fu,Zhenglin Wan,Pengfei Zhou,Wangbo Zhao,Muqing Jian,Xueyi Zhang,Yang You,Ivor Tsang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SpikeOPD：一种用于自回归脉冲神经网络（SNN）的稳态在线策略蒸馏框架。针对 ANN-to-SNN 迁移中"固定语料前缀蒸馏 vs. 自回归自生成前缀推理"的前缀来源不匹配问题，SpikeOPD 在自生成前缀上继续教师监督，同时通过匹配前缀策略锚定与逐层脉冲正则化维持 rollout 稳定性。在 0.125B、0.35B、1.3B 三个规模上，相比对应 KD SNN 平均准确率分别提升 0.8、1.7、2.9 个百分点，并保持稀疏计算特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 脉冲神经网络（SNN）凭借稀疏编码与事件驱动计算，为能耗友好的语言建模提供了路径，但从零训练有能力的脉冲语言模型非常困难。
2. 务实的替代方案是通过知识蒸馏（KD）实现 ANN-to-SNN 迁移，由预训练 ANN 教师监督 SNN 学生。
3. 现有迁移方法在固定的语料前缀上蒸馏，而自回归推理则基于模型自生成的前缀进行条件生成，二者之间存在前缀来源不匹配（prefix-source mismatch）。
4. 该不匹配表现为两个方面：（a）与 ANN 教师的输出策略不匹配；（b）自生成前缀与匹配语料前缀之间的内部脉冲动力学漂移。
5. 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）通过在自生成前缀上持续进行教师监督，提供了缓解上述两类问题的自然方式，但作者发现仅靠在线策略覆盖并不能保证稳定适应——朴素变体 Vanilla OPD 在压力测试中出现延迟的 rollout-反馈崩塌，这直接驱动了稳定化机制的设计动机。

Method: SpikeOPD 是一个包含三重机制的稳态在线策略蒸馏框架：

1. 在线策略教师修正（Online-policy teacher correction）：在 SNN 自生成的前缀上继续施加 ANN 教师的 full-KL 散度修正，直接针对自生成前缀下的分布错配，降低输出策略不匹配。

2. 匹配前缀策略锚定（Matched-prefix policy anchoring）：在同一批前缀上，将策略偏离度约束到冻结的参考 SNN（frozen reference SNN）上，防止在线适应过程中策略漂移过远，从而抑制 rollout 稳定性退化。

3. 逐层脉冲正则化（Layerwise spike regularization）：对每一层的放电率偏差施加约束，限制在线策略适应期间的 firing-rate 偏离，缓解内部脉冲动力学漂移。

作为对照，作者还评估了仅教师监督的 Vanilla OPD（teacher-only full-KL 变体），并设计了受控压力测试（controlled stress test）以暴露其不稳定性。实验覆盖 0.125B、0.35B、1.3B 三个模型规模，与对应的 KD SNN 基线进行对比。

Result: 1. Vanilla OPD（仅教师监督的 full-KL 在线策略蒸馏）在受控压力测试中出现延迟的 rollout-反馈崩塌（delayed rollout-feedback collapse），表明在线策略覆盖本身并不能保证稳定适应。
2. 加入锚定与脉冲正则化后的 SpikeOPD 显著优于对应 KD SNN：在 0.125B、0.35B、1.3B 三个规模上，平均准确率分别提升 0.8、1.7、2.9 个百分点。
3. 性能增益随模型规模增大而单调放大，说明该框架具有良好的可扩展性。
4. 在准确率提升的同时，SpikeOPD 保持了与基线相当的稀疏计算画像（sparse-compute profiles），即未牺牲 SNN 赖以节能的稀疏特性。

Conclusion: 1. 核心发现：ANN-to-SNN 迁移中的前缀来源不匹配是真实且可测量的问题，且其两种表现形式（输出策略不匹配与内部脉冲动力学漂移）确实需要通过在线策略覆盖来缓解；但仅靠在线策略覆盖会带来训练不稳定（延迟崩塌），因此稳定性约束是必需的，而非可选项。
2. 方法论结论：SpikeOPD 证明"在线策略教师信号 + 匹配前缀策略锚定 + 逐层脉冲正则化"三者组合能够实现稳定的自回归 SNN 蒸馏，兼顾分布对齐与训练稳定性。
3. 实用性意义：相比从零训练 SNN 的高难度，SpikeOPD 为在保持能耗优势的条件下获得有竞争力的脉冲语言模型提供了可扩展的工程路径；增益随规模递增意味着该路线在更大模型上的价值更加凸显。
4. 局限与展望：本文的规模上限为 1.3B，稀疏计算画像的具体数值未披露；后续可探索更大规模验证、锚定强度的自适应调节，以及将脉冲正则化与稀疏计算度量直接对齐。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) offer a path to energy-efficient language modeling through sparse encoding and event-driven computation, but training capable spiking language models from scratch remains difficult. A practical alternative is ANN-to-SNN migration through knowledge distillation (KD), where a pretrained artificial neural network (ANN) teacher supervises an SNN student. Existing migration approaches distill on fixed corpus prefixes, whereas autoregressive inference conditions on self-generated prefixes, creating prefix-source mismatch. It manifests as output-policy mismatch with the ANN teacher and internal spiking-dynamics drift between self-generated and matched corpus prefixes. On-policy distillation (OPD) offers a natural way to mitigate both manifestations by continuing teacher supervision on self-generated prefixes. We evaluate a teacher-only full-KL variant, Vanilla OPD, via a controlled stress test and observe it may suffer from delayed rollout-feedback collapse. This result shows that on-policy coverage alone does not ensure stable adaptation. Motivated by these findings, we propose SpikeOPD, a stable on-policy distillation framework for autoregressive SNNs that learns from self-generated prefixes while maintaining rollout stability. It applies full-KL teacher correction to reduce output-policy mismatch, while matched-prefix policy anchoring constrains policy departure from the frozen reference SNN on the same prefixes. Layerwise spike regularization further limits firing-rate deviations during on-policy adaptation. Across three model scales, SpikeOPD improves average accuracy over the corresponding KD SNNs by 0.8, 1.7, and 2.9 points at 0.125B, 0.35B, and 1.3B, respectively, while preserving their sparse-compute profiles.

</details>


### [211] [CoRe-MoE: Compact Reusable MoE for Continual Multimodal Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27867)
*Runze Liu,Naibin Gu,Mingxu Ai,Yuqing Li,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 CoRe-MoE（Compact Reusable MoE），一种面向持续多模态指令微调的参数高效混合专家框架。核心思想是：通过对任务特定的 LoRA 更新进行 SVD 分析，发现不同任务的输入/输出方向子空间存在大量重叠，从而提取可复用的方向基（direction bases），后续任务仅需训练紧凑的坐标专家（coordinate experts）和任务特定的低秩路由器（low-rank routers）。在两个代表性 MLLM 上，CoRe-MoE 相比最强基线最终平均性能提升最多 5.90 分，同时后续任务可训练参数不足顺序 LoRA 的 1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续多模态指令微调要求多模态大语言模型在顺序学习新任务的同时保持已有知识。LoRA-MoE 通过引入基于专家（expert）的容量提供了有前景的解决方案，但为每个任务反复学习和维护完整的 LoRA 专家导致了巨大的参数开销。由此引出核心问题：对每个新任务而言，完整的专家扩展是否真的必要？作者通过对任务特定 LoRA 更新矩阵的 SVD 分析发现，不同任务的输入侧和输出侧 LoRA 方向子空间之间存在大量重叠，任务特定的适配主要通过在这些子空间上的轻量坐标来表示，从而否定了"每个新任务都需要完整专家扩展"的假设。

Method: CoRe-MoE 的方法分为两个阶段：（1）初始阶段——构建初始专家库（initial expert bank），并从中提取可复用的输入侧和输出侧方向基（reusable input/output direction bases）；（2）后续任务阶段——针对每个新任务，仅训练紧凑的坐标专家（compact coordinate experts，即在预提取的方向基上学习坐标系数）以及任务特定的低秩路由器（task-specific low-rank routers）。这一设计的关键洞察来自 SVD 分析：任务特定 LoRA 更新的 SVD 表明，不同任务的低秩方向在输入/输出子空间中高度重叠，因此无需为每个任务分配完整的独立专家，而是可以共享底层方向基，仅用少量参数（坐标）来编码任务特有的适配信息。

Result: 在两个代表性多模态大语言模型（MLLM）上进行实验验证。结果表明，CoRe-MoE 在最终平均性能上比最强竞争基线高出最多 5.90 分，同时在后续任务中使用的可训练参数量不足顺序 LoRA 方案的 1%。该结果同时证明了性能优势和参数效率的大幅提升。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 完整专家扩展对于每个新任务并非必要。通过 SVD 驱动的可复用方向基 + 紧凑坐标专家 + 低秩路由器的设计，CoRe-MoE 在持续多模态指令微调场景中实现了显著的性能提升（+5.90 分）和极低的参数开销（<1%），为高效、可扩展的持续学习提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Continual multimodal instruction tuning requires multimodal large language models to acquire new task abilities sequentially while preserving previously learned knowledge. LoRA-MoE provides a promising solution by introducing expert-based capacity, but repeatedly learning and maintaining full LoRA experts leads to substantial parameter overhead. This raises a natural question: is full expert expansion necessary for every new task? To answer it, we analyze the SVD of task-specific LoRA updates and observe substantial overlap in their input- and output-side LoRA direction subspaces, with task-specific adaptation largely captured by lightweight coordinates over these subspaces. Motivated by this observation, we propose CoRe-MoE, a Compact Reusable MoE framework for parameter-efficient continual multimodal instruction tuning. CoRe-MoE extracts reusable input- and output-side direction bases from an initial expert bank, and for subsequent tasks trains only compact coordinate experts together with task-specific low-rank routers. Experiments on two representative MLLMs show that CoRe-MoE improves final average performance over the strongest competing baseline by up to 5.90 points, while using less than 1% of the trainable parameters required by sequential LoRA for later tasks. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/runzezz/CoRe-MoE.

</details>


### [212] [See, Hypothesize, Validate: Multimodal Agentic Framework for Discovering Governing PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27869)
*Sarang Manoj Pekhale,Amartya Roy,Rajat Sarkar,Souvik Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAGE提出了一种基于多模态智能体协作的PDE发现框架，通过"观察-假设-证伪"式置信度驱动的假设验证循环，使用四个角色专用智能体（微分观测者、现象学提取器、定律合成器、方程仲裁者）无需预定义库即可从观测数据中发现PDE。在标准PDE测试集上实现8/8精确结构恢复，7/8系统最低系数误差，误差改善最高达4个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测数据中发现PDE是跨学科核心挑战。现有方法存在固有局限：稀疏回归和符号回归依赖预定义库，难以扩展；LLM方法受限于幻觉和噪声敏感性；各方法均缺乏迭代精化能力和结构化验证机制。作者旨在构建一个兼具多模态感知、无库生成、显式置信度门控和拒绝验证能力的端到端智能体框架，模拟科学发现的"观察→假设→证伪"循环。

Method: MAGE（Multimodal Agentic Governing Equation Discovery）采用四智能体协同架构，围绕"置信度治理的假设验证循环"组织：
1. **微分观测者（Differential Observer）**：计算数据导数并生成诊断可视化，提供定量信息；
2. **现象学提取器（Phenomenology Extractor）**：VLM驱动，从多模态诊断中提取定性线索（如空间模式、衰减特征等）；
3. **定律合成器（Governing Law Synthesizer）**：LLM驱动，基于提取的定性线索提议候选方程，不依赖预定义库；
4. **方程仲裁者（Equation Arbiter）**：拟合系数并分配置信度评分，执行显式接受/拒绝协议。
发现过程迭代执行上述循环，直至最高候选方程的置信度超过用户指定阈值。核心创新在于：(a)多模态信息融合（数值+可视化+定性描述）；(b)无库方程生成；(c)置信度门控的迭代精炼；(d)接受-拒绝验证协议。

Result: 在经典PDE测试套件（共8个系统）上：
- **结构恢复**：8/8 精确结构恢复；
- **系数精度**：7/8系统取得最低系数误差；
- **误差改善**：最高达4个数量级，几何平均改善约3个数量级；
- **复杂几何**：在两种复杂几何下正确恢复预期算子；
- **实验室数据**：在一条真实传感器记录上选择三次恢复力模型，留出集R²=0.98538。
对比方法（稀疏回归、符号回归、LLM基方法等）在结构和系数误差上均被显著超越。

Conclusion: MAGE展示了结构化智能体推理在无库PDE发现中的有效性，其置信度驱动的迭代验证循环和多模态融合策略显著提升了方程发现的准确性和鲁棒性。然而，论文也指出更广泛的泛化能力（如不同噪声水平、未知物理系统、大规模数据等场景）仍有待进一步评估。该工作为AI驱动的科学研究提供了从"库约束搜索"向"开放式假设生成"范式转变的可行路径。

Abstract: Discovering governing partial differential equations (PDEs) from observational data remains a core challenge across the sciences. Existing sparse-regression, symbolic-regression, and LLM-based approaches can be constrained by predefined libraries, noise sensitivity, hallucination, or limited iterative refinement. We introduce \textbf{MAGE} (\textbf{M}ultimodal \textbf{A}gentic \textbf{G}overning \textbf{E}quation Discovery), an agentic framework that organizes PDE discovery as a \textit{confidence governed hypothesis validation loop} inspired by the scientific cycle of observation, hypothesis, and falsification. Four role-specialized agents collaborate: a \textit{Differential Observer} computing derivatives and diagnostic visualizations; a VLM-powered \textit{Phenomenology Extractor} distilling qualitative cues from multimodal diagnostics; an LLM-driven \textit{Governing Law Synthesizer} proposing candidates without a predefined library; and an \textit{Equation Arbiter} fitting coefficients and assigning confidence scores. Discovery iterates until the top candidate clears a user-specified threshold, providing a structured process with an explicit accept-reject protocol. On the evaluated canonical PDE suite, MAGE obtains \textbf{8/8} exact structural recovery and the lowest coefficient error among the compared methods on \textbf{7/8} systems, with improvements of up to \textbf{4 orders of magnitude} and a geometric-mean improvement of approximately \textbf{3 orders of magnitude}. The pipeline also recovers the expected operators in two complex geometries and, on one laboratory sensor record, selects a cubic restoring-force model with held-out $R^2=0.98538$. These results support further study of structured agentic reasoning for library-free governing-law discovery, while broader generalization remains to be evaluated.

</details>


### [213] [HyQuant: Hybrid-Precision Quantization for LLM Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27875)
*Jiatong Ding,Bingxin Xing,Yu Zhang,Dian Ding,Xiaodong Yi,Xianbin Ouyang,Feihu Zhou,Kun Zhang,Zhenyu Guo,Hao Pan,Guangtao Xue,Yiming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyQuant是一种针对LLM注意力模块的高效混合量化框架，通过保留少量关键token（垂直线token和局部窗口token）为高精度、其余上下文量化为低比特的方式，在极低比特位宽下实现了近乎无损的精度，同时兼顾了显存和硬件效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化被广泛用于降低LLM训练和推理成本，但注意力模块在极低比特位宽下常引入显著误差导致性能下降。现有方法主要依赖平滑技术处理离群值，难以在精度与效率间取得良好平衡。论文旨在探索一种更优的混合量化设计，以在保持推理效率的同时将量化误差控制在可接受范围内。

Method: HyQuant的核心思路是"混合精度"策略：(1)识别对精度至关重要的区域——利用轻量级的"垂直线感知注意力模式信号"筛选出少量垂直线token和局部滑动窗口token，将它们保持为高精度（full precision）；(2)Pre-fill阶段：使用混合精度量化注意力算子，对垂直线token和局部滑动窗口保留全精度，其余上下文进行低比特量化；(3)Decode阶段：将相同原则应用于KV-cache压缩，并将KV反量化与注意力计算进行融合，以减少访存开销、提升硬件利用率。整体设计简洁，开销有限。

Result: 在多种任务、模型和数据集上的实验表明，HyQuant以极其简单的架构设计实现了近乎无损（nearly lossless）的精度，同时展现出优秀的显存与推理效率，验证了混合量化用于LLM注意力模块的可行性与实用性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过对注意力模块中少数关键区域（垂直线token和局部窗口）保留高精度、其余部分低比特量化，并配合KV-cache压缩与反量化融合的优化，HyQuant证明了混合量化是一种在极低比特位宽下兼顾精度与效率的实用方案，为LLM推理优化提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Quantization has been widely adopted in LLM training and inference to reduce cost and improve efficiency. However, low-bit quantization of the \emph{attention} module often introduces large errors at very low bit-widths, causing performance degradation. Existing methods mainly rely on smoothing techniques to handle outliers, while we propose a hybrid quantization design to better balance accuracy and efficiency. Specifically, we propose \textbf{HyQuant}, an efficient hybrid quantization framework for LLM attention. HyQuant quantizes most attention states into low-bit formats while retaining a small set of vertical-line tokens and local-window states in high precision. These accuracy-critical regions are selected using lightweight vertical-line-aware attention-pattern signals, reducing quantization error with limited overhead. In the Prefill stage, HyQuant uses a hybrid-precision quantized attention operator that preserves vertical-line tokens and a local sliding window in full precision while quantizing the remaining context. In the Decode stage, HyQuant applies the same principle to KV-cache compression and fuses KV dequantization with attention computation to improve memory and hardware efficiency. Across diverse tasks, models, and datasets, HyQuant maintains nearly lossless accuracy with an extremely simple design, demonstrating the efficiency and practical feasibility of hybrid quantization for LLM attention. Code is available at: https://github.com/jerrysfls/HyQuant .

</details>


### [214] [Resource Constraints and Performance in Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27886)
*Amaz Salman,Malka Halgamuge,Teo Susnjak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对两个智能体系统 OpenClaw 与 NanoBot 进行配对基准评测，结果显示两者在完整任务完成率上无统计学显著差异（31% vs 25%，95% CI [-3, 15]），但 OpenClaw 在资源和时间消耗上显著更高（几何均值比：墙钟时间 2.98x，峰值内存 19.44x）。NanoBot 在部分完成方面表现更优（43% vs 26%），但其在多数主导情形中属于更廉价的"联合失败"。论文强调智能体评测须将能力与资源度量绑定到执行级的溯源记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI 系统日益自主化，将语言模型与工具、记忆、状态管理和多步执行结合的智能体（agentic）系统成为关键范式。这些机制既塑造任务能力，也带来运营负担。然而，当前对智能体系统的评估缺乏统一且严格的配对基准方法——需要将任务完成结果与具体执行轨迹、资源消耗紧密关联，并明确评分来源（scoring provenance），才能得出可信的比较结论。

Method: 1. **配对基准框架（Paired Benchmark）**：对同一组提示词分别让 OpenClaw 和 NanoBot 执行，计算完整任务完成率（full task completion rate）。
2. **分层评估**：
   - **主基准层（Primary Benchmark）**：仅统计完整完成率，使用 task-bootstrap 方法构建 95% 置信区间。
   - **仪器化详测层（Instrumented Detailed Layer）**：对配对的子集提示词进行更细粒度测量，记录部分完成、完整完成、墙钟时间（wall time）和峰值内存（peak memory）。
3. **资源与执行追踪**：在每个提示词粒度上记录执行耗时和内存消耗，计算几何均值比。
4. **主导性分析**：统计各系统在各提示词上取得更优结果（部分或完整完成）的次数，并区分真正的完成与"更廉价的联合失败"。
5. **跨层标签一致性检查**：比较两个证据层中输出标签是否一致，揭示评测方法论的潜在偏差。

Result: 1. **主基准层**：OpenClaw 完整完成率 31%，NanoBot 25%，差距 6 个百分点，95% 任务 bootstrap 区间 [-3, 15]，**无统计学显著优势**。
2. **仪器化详测层**：
   - 完整完成率两者均为 26%。
   - 部分完成：NanoBot 43% vs OpenClaw 26%。
3. **资源消耗**：
   - OpenClaw 在 83% 的提示词上耗时更长。
   - OpenClaw 在**所有**提示词上峰值内存更高。
   - 几何均值比：墙钟时间 2.98x，峰值内存 19.44x。
4. **主导性分析**：
   - 在至少一方取得部分/完整完成的 10 个提示词中，NanoBot 在 8 个上弱占优。
   - 但在全部 23 个提示词中，NanoBot 的 18 次主导中有 10 次属于"更廉价的联合失败"（即双方都未完成任务，但 NanoBot 消耗更少资源）。
5. **跨层标签不一致**：两个证据层对同一输出的标签存在差异，说明智能体评测必须将能力与资源度量连接到具体执行溯源。

Conclusion: 本文的核心结论是：迈向更自主 AI 的进展不应仅以任务完成率的数值高低来评判，而应通过**已验证的任务完成、可观察的资源使用记录、以及将每个结果与其产生执行链路相连的溯源机制**来综合评估。具体而言：

1. **配对基准 + 分层评估**是必要的方法论：单一指标（如完整完成率）可能掩盖资源代价差异；分层设计（主基准 + 仪器化详测）能同时揭示能力与效率图景。
2. **统计显著性不可忽视**：主基准中 6 个百分点的差距经 bootstrap 检验后无法确认优势，表明样本量不足时不宜得出排名结论。
3. **资源效率是隐性但关键的评估维度**：OpenClaw 在内存和耗时上的巨大劣势（19.44x 峰值内存）在实际部署中可能是决定性瓶颈。
4. **"联合失败"需被识别和标注**：主导性分析中大量"更廉价的联合失败"说明，当双方均未完成任务时，资源消耗较低的"失败"并不代表能力更强。
5. **评测溯源（scoring provenance）是可信性的基石**：跨层标签不一致问题凸显了将每个评分结果绑定到其产生执行记录的重要性。

总体而言，本文为智能体系统的**评测方法论**贡献了一套实践框架，强调能力与资源的双维度评估及其执行级溯源。

Abstract: Progress toward more autonomous AI increasingly depends on agentic systems that combine a language model with tools, memory, state management, and multi-step execution. These mechanisms shape both task capability and operational burden. We compare OpenClaw and NanoBot as complete agentic systems using a paired primary benchmark and a more detailed instrumented subset of paired prompts. In the primary benchmark, the rate of full task completion was 31% for OpenClaw and 25% for NanoBot, a six-percentage-point difference with a 95% task-bootstrap interval from -3 to 15 percentage points, providing no statistically established full-completion advantage for either system. In the instrumented layer, both systems achieved 26% full completion, while NanoBot reached at least partial completion on 43% of prompts compared with 26% for OpenClaw. OpenClaw took longer on 83% of prompts and had a higher recorded peak-memory value on every prompt, with geometric mean ratios of 2.98 for wall time and 19.44 for peak memory. Among the ten detailed-layer prompts on which at least one system achieved partial or full completion, NanoBot weakly dominated on eight; across all 23 prompts, however, ten of its eighteen dominance cases were cheaper joint failures. Outcome labels differ across the two evidence layers, showing why agent-system evaluation should connect capability and resource measurements to attempt-level execution and scoring provenance. These findings show that progress toward more autonomous AI should be evaluated through verified task completion, observed resource use and records linking each result to the execution that produced it.

</details>


### [215] [Rubric-to-Code Credit Assignment for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27906)
*Rui Jin,Jikai Chen,Yihan Chen,Hao Zhou,Demin Zhu,Kaichen Yang,Dong Wang,Chenyi Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RCCA（Rubric-to-Code Credit Assignment）框架，将评分标准级别的反馈转化为代码的局部化优化信号，解决了传统GRPO在代码生成中信用分配过弱的问题。生成的模型Ling-RCCA-Flash在MiniAppBench上得分41.25，超越Claude Opus 4.5；在ArtifactsBench上得分76.19，超越GPT-5，确立了新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式Web应用生成需要从自然语言描述中生成可用的HTML、CSS和JavaScript应用。与常规代码生成不同，应用质量取决于多个面向用户的功能需求，这些需求通常与局部代码区域（如事件处理器、状态更新、DOM片段或CSS选择器）相关。然而，标准GRPO将这些结构化输出坍缩为单一的序列级奖励，并将优势函数均匀应用到所有token上，严重削弱了信用分配能力——无法精确定位哪些代码区域负责哪些功能需求，导致优化信号分散且低效。

Method: RCCA框架包含以下核心组件：
1. **基于显式功能评分标准的训练任务构建**：将应用功能需求分解为明确的评分标准（rubrics），作为训练任务的评估依据。
2. **分层奖励机制（Hierarchical Reward）**：将反馈分为四个层级——格式失败、源代码失败、运行时失败和功能失败，分别对应不同粒度的错误类型。
3. **文本归因与代码段对齐**：将评估器生成的文本归因（textual attributions）与负责相应功能的代码区域（code spans）和生成的token进行对齐，实现将评分标准级别的反馈精确转化为局部化的优化信号。
4. 基于上述机制，采用强化学习进行模型微调，得到Ling-RCCA-Flash模型。

Result: 1. **MiniAppBench**：Ling-RCCA-Flash得分41.25，相比Ling-3.0-Flash提升32.20分，并略微超越Claude Opus 4.5。
2. **ArtifactsBench**：得分76.19，相比SFT模型提升4.48分；在官方排行榜设置下，超越GPT-5的分数3.64分，确立新SOTA。
3. 结果表明该方法的实现层面提升具有可迁移性。

Conclusion: RCCA通过分层奖励和文本归因-代码段对齐机制，有效解决了代码生成中信用分配不均的问题，实现了从评分标准到代码区域的精确反馈传导。实验结果验证了该方法在交互式Web应用生成任务上的显著有效性，且在多个基准上达到或超越当前最优模型，证明了从功能需求到局部代码优化的对齐策略可带来可迁移的实现级增益。

Abstract: Interactive web application generation requires models to produce usable HTML, CSS, and JavaScript applications from natural language requests. Unlike conventional code generation, application quality depends on multiple user-facing functional requirements, each often tied to localized code regions such as event handlers, state updates, DOM fragments, or CSS selectors. Standard GRPO collapses these structured outcomes into a single sequence-level reward and applies the resulting advantage uniformly to all tokens, weakening credit assignment. We propose \textbf{Rubric-to-Code Credit Assignment} (RCCA), a reinforcement learning framework that converts rubric-level functional feedback into localized optimization signals over generated code. RCCA builds training tasks around explicit functional rubrics, uses a hierarchical reward to separate format, source-code, runtime, and functional failures, and aligns evaluator-generated textual attributions with responsible code spans and generated tokens. The resulting model, \textbf{Ling-RCCA-Flash}, scores 41.25 on MiniAppBench, improving Ling-3.0-Flash by 32.20 points and slightly surpassing Claude Opus 4.5. It also reaches 76.19 on ArtifactsBench, improving the SFT model by 4.48 points and establishing a new top score under the official ArtifactsBench leaderboard setting by surpassing the GPT-5 score by 3.64 points, suggesting transferable implementation-level gains.

</details>


### [216] [AI Alignment through a Game-theoretic Lens: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27910)
*Yanan Cai,Zhongrui Zhao,Zhigang Lu,Ickjai Lee,Wei Emma Zhang,Minhui Xue,Yihong Zhang,Shuchao Pang,Wei Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述以博弈论视角审视AI对齐问题，围绕偏好多样性、对齐优先级与时间动态三大挑战梳理近期进展，明确指出博弈论框架在对齐研究中真正有效之处、适用边界较为松散之处，以及构建鲁棒、自适应、可验证AI系统所面临的剩余挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型及能力日益增强的AI智能体被部署于高风险场景，使AI与复杂人类价值观对齐成为核心挑战。现有对齐方法虽在提升有用性、无害性与可控性方面取得成效，却难以捕捉现实世界中情境依赖、非传递性且由多方动态交互所塑造的真实偏好。传统对齐方法的局限性呼唤引入更具结构性的分析工具，博弈论正是契合这一需求的理论框架——它天然处理多方策略性交互、偏好不一致及动态决策问题。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，以博弈论为分析透镜组织近期研究进展。具体围绕博弈论的关键要素（如参与者、策略、收益结构、均衡概念、机制设计等）对已有文献进行分类梳理，并沿三条核心轴线综合：(1) 偏好多样性——处理多主体、情境依赖且非传递性偏好的对齐；(2) 对齐优先级——协调安全性、有用性与可控性之间的权衡冲突；(3) 时间动态——应对随时间演化的对齐目标与多方交互策略。在此基础上辨析博弈论框架的适用边界。

Result: 该综述系统性地绘制了博弈论与AI对齐的交叉图景，明确区分了三类情形：当前对齐方法确实能从博弈论分析中实质性获益的领域、博弈论框架适用性较为松散的领域，以及在迈向鲁棒、自适应与可验证AI系统过程中尚待解决的关键挑战。通过结构化梳理，为后续研究提供了理论地图与方向指引。

Conclusion: 博弈论为理解复杂、动态且多方参与的AI对齐问题提供了有力但尚不完备的分析工具。该综述揭示了现有对齐研究中对齐方法与博弈论结合的有效区与模糊区，指出在偏好多样性、优先级协调与时间动态三个维度上仍存在显著的研究空白，强调了未来需进一步发展更严格的博弈论建模与可验证的对齐机制，以实现真正稳健的AI价值观对齐。

Abstract: As large language models and increasingly capable AI agents are deployed in high-risk settings, aligning them with complex human values has become a central challenge. Existing alignment methods, while effective in improving helpfulness, harmlessness, and controllability, often struggle to capture real-world preferences that are context-dependent, non-transitive, and shaped by dynamic multi-party interactions. This survey reviews AI alignment through a game-theoretic lens. Specifically, it organizes recent progress around key game-theoretic elements and synthesizes the literature along three challenges: preference diversity, alignment priority, and temporal dynamics. This perspective clarifies where current alignment methods genuinely benefit from game-theoretic analysis, where the framework is looser, and what challenges remain in building robust, adaptive, and verifiable AI systems.

</details>


### [217] [From Documents to Reasoning: A Validated Synthetic Data Pipeline and Semantic-Aware Fine-Tuning for Financial Numerical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27919)
*Lokendra Birla,Milind Savagaonkar,Visnu Srinivasan,Sowmya Rasipuram,Shubhashis Sengupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套针对金融问答（Financial QA）的完整改进流程：通过高质量合成数据生成与基于QLoRA的中小语言模型微调，并引入新颖的评估指标和修改后的损失函数，在ConvFinQA基准上显著提升了问答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融问答是评估LLM领域能力的核心基准，涉及表格、图表和复杂文本等多种模态。现有方法存在以下痛点：(1)标准评估指标（如Exact Match）无法容许单位或格式上的微小差异，导致模型性能评估不准确、不可靠；(2)模型在多步算术推理和跨模态理解上的性能一致性仍有不足。

Method: 核心方法包含三个层面：
1. **合成数据生成与验证**：设计激进的合成问答对生成流程，加入严格的数据验证机制，确保合成QA对的相关性与正确性。
2. **模型微调**：利用量化低秩自适应（QLoRA）对较小的语言模型（SLMs）进行参数高效微调，降低训练资源需求的同时提升领域适应性。
3. **新颖评估指标与损失函数**：
   - 提出基于算术表达式匹配的评估指标——将模型输出的算术表达式与参考表达式直接比对（而非最终答案值），更真实反映模型推理能力；
   - 设计修改后的损失函数，融合语义相似度（predicted vs. reference expressions）、新型评估指标以及标准交叉熵损失，三者联合优化。

Result: 在ConvFinQA基准数据集上的实验表明：使用合成数据集微调并配合所提损失函数后，金融问答准确率获得显著提升。该结果表明：合成数据 + QLoRA微调 + 改进损失函数/评估指标的组合方案对提升金融QA系统性能有效。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献在于：(1)证明了高质量合成数据结合QLoRA微调能有效提升中小模型在金融QA上的表现；(2)提出的基于算术表达式匹配的评估指标克服了传统EM指标的局限性，更精准地衡量推理能力；(3)融合语义相似度与新型指标的修改损失函数带来了性能增益。整体方案为领域特定LLM的可靠评估与高效微调提供了可借鉴的范式。

Abstract: Financial question answering (QA) has emerged as a key benchmark for evaluating the performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) on domain-specific tasks involving complex data formats such as tables, charts, and rich textual narratives. While recent advancements have enabled models to reason across modalities and perform multi-step arithmetic operations, limitations remain in performance consistency, and evaluation reliability. In particular, standard evaluation metrics like Exact Match (EM) often fail to account for minor variations such as differences in units or formats, misleading performance assessments.
  In this work, we propose a comprehensive pipeline for improving financial QA systems through high-quality synthetic data generation and fine-tuning of smaller language models (SLMs) using Quantized Low-Rank Adaptation (QLoRA). Our pipeline includes aggressive data validation for synthetic question answer generation to ensure the relevance and correctness of synthetic question-answer pairs. We introduce a novel evaluation metric that matches answers computed from arithmetic expressions rather than ground-truth answers; providing a more accurate reflection of model reasoning capability. Furthermore, we propose a modified loss function that aligns predicted and reference expressions using semantic similarity, our novel evaluation metric and standard cross-entropy, resulting in improved performance. Experimental results on benchmark datasets, ConvFinQA demonstrate significant gains in QA accuracy after fine-tuning using synthetic dataset and proposed loss function.

</details>


### [218] [A Deep Learning-Based Stacking Ensemble Framework for Turbofan Engine Remaining Useful Life Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27940)
*Limon Bin Hossain,Md. Salehin Seyam,Md Rashedul Islam,Abdur Rahman,Md Sharifuzzaman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两级堆叠集成框架用于涡扇发动机剩余使用寿命（RUL）预测，结合LSTM、CNN、CNN-LSTM、CNN-GRU四种异构深度学习基学习器，并通过XGBoost元学习器融合其折外预测，在NASA C-MAPSS基准（FD001和FD003子集）上取得显著优于基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在航空安全关键领域中，单一深度学习模型存在固有偏差，难以全面捕捉发动机复杂的退化模式。为了提高剩余使用寿命（RUL）预测的精度与鲁棒性，亟需一种能够有效整合多种模型优势、抵消个体偏差的集成学习框架，以支撑航空器的预测与健康管理（PHM）。

Method: 1. 两级堆叠集成框架：第一级由四个异构深度学习基学习器组成——LSTM（擅长序列建模）、CNN（擅长特征提取）、CNN-LSTM（融合时空特征）、CNN-GRU（兼顾梯度记忆与卷积特征）；
2. 基学习器生成折外（out-of-fold）预测，避免数据泄漏；
3. 第二级采用XGBoost作为元学习器，对基学习器的预测进行加权融合，捕捉复杂的非线性退化模式并缓解单模型偏差；
4. 实验平台为NASA C-MAPSS基准数据集，选取FD001和FD003两个子集进行评估；
5. 辅以特征相关性分析、残差诊断和训练收敛曲线分析来验证模型鲁棒性。

Result: 1. FD001子集：RMSE=9.989，MAE=7.081，R²=0.899；
2. FD003子集：RMSE=8.613，MAE=5.195，R²=0.906；
3. 相比最佳基线TCAT（FD001 RMSE=11.12，FD003 RMSE=11.02），RMSE分别降低10.2%和21.8%；
4. 特征相关性分析表明所选基学习器之间存在合理的互补性；
5. 残差诊断显示预测误差分布良好，训练收敛曲线验证了训练过程的稳定性与模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文验证了堆叠集成方法在涡扇发动机RUL预测中的有效性与优越性。通过多异构基学习器与XGBoost元学习器的两级融合架构，该方法能够更全面地捕捉复杂退化模式，显著降低预测误差。研究结果对航空器预测与健康管理领域具有重要的工程参考价值，为安全关键应用的智能维护提供了可靠的技术路径。

Abstract: This study proposes a two-level stacking ensemble framework for Remaining Useful Life (RUL) prediction of turbofan engines, evaluated on the NASA C-MAPSS benchmark using the FD001 and FD003 subsets. The framework integrates four heterogeneous deep learning base learners: Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), Convolutional Neural Network (CNN), CNN-LSTM, and CNN-GRU, whose out-of-fold predictions are combined by an XGBoost meta-learner to capture complex degradation patterns while mitigating individual model biases. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that the stacking ensemble achieves superior predictive performance, with Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) of 9.989 and 8.613, Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 7.081 and 5.195, and R-squared values of 0.899 and 0.906 for FD001 and FD003, respectively. Compared to the best-reported baseline (TCAT: RMSE 11.12 and 11.02), the proposed method achieves RMSE reductions of 10.2 percent and 21.8 percent for FD001 and FD003, respectively. Feature correlation analysis, residual diagnostics, and training convergence curves validate the model's robustness. These findings underscore the efficacy of stacking ensemble methods for prognostics and health management in safety-critical aerospace applications.

</details>


### [219] [CASTANET: Causality-Aware Spatio-Temporal Adversarial Network Using Traffic Incident Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27942)
*Toshiya Kitahara,Ryu Shirakami,Koh Takeuchi,Hisashi Kashima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CASTANET，一种融合时空图神经网络与因果处理效应估计的交通拥堵预测方法，专门针对突发事件（如事故、道路损坏）引发的非周期性拥堵预测难题，在东京真实交通与事故数据上相比最优基线整体RMSE降低4.0%，事件条件评估下降低10.1%，严重拥堵场景下最高降低14.55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非周期性交通拥堵（由突发事故、道路损坏等事件驱动）对高级智能交通系统至关重要，但预测极为困难，原因有三：(1)事件极其稀疏；(2)事件在特定时间和地点发生，存在强烈时空偏差；(3)不同交通语境下事件的影响具有异质性。现有深度学习时空预测方法虽然在周期性交通预测上表现优异，但未显式纳入事件记录，且无法处理事件发生时空分布偏倚问题，导致非周期性拥堵预测性能受限。

Method: CASTANET将时空图神经网络（STGNN）与因果处理效应估计（Causal Treatment Effect Estimation）相结合。STGNN负责捕获交通流的时空依赖关系（路网结构作为图、时间维度建模动态演化），而因果处理效应估计则用于显式建模突发事件对交通状态的因果影响，同时通过因果推断框架缓解事件记录中的选择偏差（selection bias）——即事件并非随机分布在时空域，而是集中于特定时空位置，传统方法容易将这种偏倚误认为因果效应。该方法利用事件记录作为"处理变量"（treatment），在预测过程中量化事件对拥堵的净因果贡献。

Result: 在东京真实世界交通数据与事故记录上的实验表明：(1)整体RMSE相比最优基线降低4.0%；(2)在事件条件评估（incident-conditioned evaluation）下RMSE降低10.1%；(3)在严重拥堵场景下RMSE降低幅度高达14.55%。这表明CASTANET在事件相关拥堵预测中效果尤为显著，验证了因果处理效应估计框架在缓解时空选择偏差方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将因果推断方法（处理效应估计）与深度学习时空建模（图神经网络）相结合，能够有效解决突发事件驱动的非周期性交通拥堵预测难题。核心贡献在于：通过因果框架显式建模事件影响并缓解事件记录的时空选择偏差，从而显著提升非周期性拥堵预测精度，尤其在严重拥堵场景下增益最为突出。这为智能交通系统中突发事件感知的交通预测提供了新的技术范式，也为因果机器学习在实际时空数据任务中的应用提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: Predicting non-periodic traffic congestion caused by sudden incidents (e.g., accidents and road damage) is crucial for advanced intelligent transportation systems. However, incident-driven congestion is difficult to forecast because incidents are extremely sparse, occur at specific times and locations, and have heterogeneous impacts depending on the traffic context. While recent deep learning approaches have significantly improved periodic traffic forecasting, their performance on non-periodic congestion remains limited, partly because incident records are not explicitly incorporated and their occurrence is strongly biased in space and time. To address these challenges, we propose CASTANET, which integrates spatio-temporal graph neural networks and causal treatment effect estimation to utilize incident records while mitigating selection bias. Experiments on real-world traffic data and accident records from Tokyo, which we treat as incidents, show that CASTANET reduces RMSE by 4.0% overall compared to the best baseline and by 10.1% on incident-conditioned evaluation, with gains reaching 14.55% under severe congestion.

</details>


### [220] [Cross-Session Decomposition Attacks: Scaling Risk and Intent-Aligned Retrieval Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27945)
*Disen Liao,Yihan Wang,Freda Shi,Yaoliang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩展定律（Scaling Laws）的安全后果——跨会话分解攻击（Cross-Session Decomposition Attacks）：良性子查询在独立交互中提出，随后被重组为有害目标。论文形式化了"组合性安全风险"，证明了条件风险转移界，并通过合成实验和600意图的预训练LLM评估发现，更大的模型在固定分解-重组管道下表现出更强的有害能力提升。提出的22M参数意图对齐检索器IntentAlign-MiniLM在有害意图召回上优于更大的嵌入模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展定律通常被解读为"能力叙事"：更低的目标函数损失意味着更有用的模型。但本文关注这一机制的安全后果：当模型在跨会话场景下将分散的良性子查询组合为被禁止的目标时，模型规模越大可能带来的安全收益是否伴随更高的风险？核心动机在于揭示"能力扩展"与"安全风险"之间的耦合关系。

Method: 1. **形式化框架**：定义"组合性安全风险"，将跨会话分解攻击建模为从分散子查询到禁止目标的重组过程；
2. **理论分析**：证明条件风险转移界——当参考环境中已存在分散的风险重建证据时，部署模型与参考模型之间的组合风险差距可由模型在允许子查询上的超额损失控制；
3. **合成实验**：设计数据留存（withholding）实验，验证更宽的Transformer是否对从未逐字出现但可从注入支撑事实恢复的指令分配更低损失；
4. **实证评估**：在600个意图的预训练LLM上评估，对比Qwen3和Gemma3系列不同规模模型的有害能力提升；
5. **防御方案**：提出IntentAlign-MiniLM——一个22M参数的意图对齐检索器，用于拦截恶意意图检索。

Result: 1. **理论结果**：风险转移界成立，模型在允许子查询上的超额损失直接控制组合风险差距；
2. **合成实验**：更宽的Transformer确实对可恢复但未逐字出现的保留指令赋予更低损失，表明缩放增强了隐含推理能力；
3. **实证评估**：更大的Qwen3和Gemma3模型成员在固定分解-重组管道下表现出更大的有害能力提升，验证了扩展定律的安全代价；
4. **防御效果**：IntentAlign-MiniLM在保留意图检索上超越远大于它的嵌入模型，并在测试的护栏中取得最佳学习检索器的有害召回率。

Conclusion: 扩展定律不仅是能力故事，也是安全风险故事。模型规模增大在提升组合推理能力的同时，放大了跨会话分解攻击的潜在危害。论文证明该风险在理论上可通过模型损失差距控制，并通过实验验证。作为防御，小型意图对齐检索器（如IntentAlign-MiniLM）能以远低于主流嵌入模型的参数量实现更优的恶意意图检测，表明"以轻量防御对抗重量风险"的可行性。

Abstract: Scaling laws are usually read as a capability story: lower language-modeling loss yields more useful models. We study a safety consequence of this mechanism in \emph{cross-session decomposition attacks}, where benign-looking subqueries are asked across independent interactions and later recomposed toward a forbidden objective. We formalize this setting as \emph{compositional safety risk} and prove a conditional risk-transfer bound: when the reference environment already contains dispersed evidence for a risky reconstruction, the gap between deployed composed risk and reference composed risk is controlled by the model's excess loss on allowed subqueries. Synthetic withholding experiments show that wider transformers assign lower loss to held-out instructions that never appear verbatim in training but are recoverable from injected supporting facts. A 600-intent pretrained-LLM evaluation shows that larger Qwen3 and Gemma3 family members can yield greater harmful-capability uplift under a fixed decomposition-composition pipeline. As a defense, IntentAlign-MiniLM, our 22M-parameter intent-aligned retriever, outperforms much larger embedding models on held-out intent retrieval and yields the best learned-retriever harmful recall across tested guardrails. Code is available in \href{https://github.com/liaodisen/Cross-Session-Decomposition-Attacks}{our GitHub repository}.

</details>


### [221] [The Illusion of $\textit{What If}$: Evaluating the Breakdown of Counterfactual Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27953)
*Yucheng Wang,Yuetian Du,Zhengyi Liu,Rongyu Zhang,Bing Zhao,Boyu Yang,Ming Kong,Lin Qu,Hu Wei,Jie Liu,Qiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WhatIfBench——一个面向开放域、开放形式、长时程反事实因果推理的诊断性基准，包含220个跨STEM、HSS及混合场景的what-if问题，并提出PRISM评估框架，通过构建响应语义因果图并联合应用过程指标和评分表指标来评估自由文本回答的因果推理质量。对六个前沿LLM的评估表明，最强模型最终得分仅为64.62%，揭示了流畅的反事实叙述往往掩盖脆弱的因果过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实推理要求模型超越已观测世界，解释改变的条件如何传导至下游后果。然而现有基准主要局限于变量固定或单一正确答案的有限场景，忽视了需要因果过程评估的开放域场景。这导致对LLM在开放形式、长时程反事实推理能力上的评估存在盲区。

Method: 1）构建WhatIfBench：设计220个跨STEM（理工科）、HSS（人文社科）和混合场景的开放域反事实问题，覆盖长时程因果链。2）提出PRISM评估框架：首先将自然语言解释转换为响应衍生语义因果图（Response-Derived Semantic Causal Graph），包含事件、状态和机制三类节点；然后在图上联合应用两个指标——过程指标（Process Metric）评估图级因果有效性，评分表指标（Rubric Metric）评估答案级解释充分性。3）使用六个前沿LLM进行系统性评估。

Result: 1）WhatIfBench远未饱和：最强模型最终得分仅64.62%，表明当前LLM在开放域反事实因果推理上仍有显著不足。2）进一步分析揭示了三个持续性问题：因果缺口（causal gaps）、前提漂移（premise drift）和拓扑碎片化（topology fragmentation），说明模型生成的流畅反事实叙述往往掩盖了脆弱的因果推理过程。

Conclusion: 当前前沿LLM在开放域反事实因果推理方面存在系统性缺陷，尤其是因果链条的完整性、前提一致性以及推理拓扑的结构化方面。WhatIfBench和PRISM为评估这类开放形式因果推理能力提供了新的工具，揭示了表面语言流畅性与深层因果推理能力之间的显著鸿沟，为未来改进因果推理能力指明了方向。

Abstract: Counterfactual reasoning requires models to reason beyond the observed world and explain how altered conditions propagate through downstream consequences. Existing benchmarks largely target bounded settings with fixed variables or single gold outcomes, overlooking open-domain scenarios requiring causal-process evaluation. To this end, we present $\textbf{WhatIfBench}$, a diagnostic benchmark for open-domain, open-form, long-horizon counterfactual causal reasoning, containing 220 what-if questions across STEM, HSS, and Hybrid scenarios. To evaluate free-form responses, we further propose $\textbf{PRISM}$, which first converts each natural-language explanation into a Response-Derived Semantic Causal Graph of events, states, and mechanisms. On top of this graph, PRISM then jointly applies a Process Metric assessing graph-level causal validity and a Rubric Metric assessing answer-level explanatory adequacy. Evaluating six frontier LLMs with this framework, we find that WhatIfBench remains far from saturated: even the strongest model reaches only a 64.62% final score. Further analysis reveals persistent causal gaps, premise drift, and topology fragmentation, suggesting that fluent counterfactual narratives often mask fragile causal processes. The benchmark, code, and evaluation scripts are available at $\href{https://github.com/zju-gt/WhatIfBench}{WhatIfBench}$.

</details>


### [222] [When Teacher Guidance Misleads: Reward-Aligned On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27960)
*Siyuan Gan,Yuhan Li,Xiran Wang,Linjian Meng,Boyan Wang,Zhen Zhao,Jing Huo,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RA-OPD（Reward-Aligned On-Policy Distillation），通过在on-policy蒸馏中过滤"误对齐"的teacher指导轨迹，使学生模型的性能与结果奖励保持一致，在数学和代码基准上显著超越标准OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy蒸馏（OPD）是一种流行的LLM后训练范式，可将teacher模型的知识高效迁移至student模型。然而，teacher模型在student生成前缀上的指导并不总是可靠的——当teacher诱导的更新方向与outcome reward（结果奖励）不一致时，即"误对齐指导"（misaligned guidance），会误导优化过程并降低模型性能。当前OPD方法未对此类不一致性进行筛选，导致student模型训练偏离正确轨迹或趋向错误轨迹。

Method: RA-OPD的核心思想是：仅保留那些使student模型向正确轨迹移动或远离错误轨迹的轨迹。具体流程如下：
1. **轨迹采样**：从student模型中采样on-policy轨迹。
2. **一致性检查**：对每条采样轨迹，计算其轨迹级蒸馏回报（trajectory-level distillation return）与outcome reward之间的一致性。
3. **过滤误对齐轨迹**：将蒸馏回报方向与outcome reward方向不一致的轨迹过滤掉，仅保留"对齐"的轨迹用于优化。
4. **零额外计算成本**：一致性检查仅需比较方向符号，无需额外模型调用或大规模推理。

该方法本质上是OPD与outcome reward之间的对齐过滤机制，不改变蒸馏框架，仅在选择哪些轨迹用于梯度更新时引入reward-aware筛选。

Result: - 实验模型：Qwen3系列与DeepSeek-R1系列模型
- 评测基准：7个数学基准 + 3个代码基准
- 核心结果：
  - RA-OPD在所有测试基准上**显著优于**标准OPD及多种OPD变体
  - 在数学推理和代码生成任务中均取得一致的性能提升
  - 性能提升来源于轨迹筛选的质量控制，而非增加计算资源

Conclusion: 1. **问题识别**：OPD中的teacher指导存在与outcome reward不一致的"误对齐"问题，这是一个被先前工作忽视的关键缺陷。
2. **方法有效性**：通过轻量级的轨迹级一致性过滤，RA-OPD以零额外计算成本显著提升了蒸馏训练的效率和质量。
3. **通用性**：该方法在Qwen3和DeepSeek-R1两个不同系列模型上均验证有效，且横跨数学和代码两类任务，表明其具有较好的跨模型与跨任务泛化能力。
4. **潜在局限性**：（1）outcome reward的质量直接影响过滤效果，reward信号不准确可能导致过度过滤；（2）过滤可能减少训练数据量，影响收敛速度；（3）方法依赖trajectory-level distillation return与outcome reward方向的一致性，对中间步骤的细粒度对齐考虑不足。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has recently emerged as a popular post-training paradigm for large language models (LLMs), providing an efficient way to transfer the knowledge and capabilities of teacher models into student models. However, teacher guidance on student-generated prefixes is not always reliable. Training should optimize the model to generate responses that are more likely to be correct, or equivalently, to get higher outcome rewards. But during OPD, the teacher model may provide guidance that discourages the student from moving toward correct trajectories or moves the student toward incorrect ones, which is misaligned with outcome reward. Such misaligned guidance is unreliable, as it would mislead the optimization process and ultimately degrade model performance. To mitigate misaligned teacher guidance, we propose Reward-Aligned On-Policy Distillation (RA-OPD). The key insight is to keep only trajectories whose induced updates move the student toward correct trajectories or discourage the student from moving toward incorrect ones. Specifically, for each sampled trajectory, RA-OPD checks whether its trajectory-level distillation return is consistent with its outcome reward and then filters out the misaligned trajectories. RA-OPD selects more reliable trajectories to improve student model performance without requiring additional computational cost. We evaluate RA-OPD on math and code benchmarks using models from the Qwen3 family and the DeepSeek-R1 family. Across seven math benchmarks and three code benchmarks, RA-OPD significantly outperforms standard OPD and other tested OPD variants.

</details>


### [223] [SABER: Stability-Aware Early Exit for LLM Reasoning via Adversarial Branch Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27963)
*Wanli Cheng,Haiya Xiang,Juntao Li,Hongling Wang,Wenliang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SABER 是一种免训练的框架，通过对抗分支探测实现稳定性感知的提前退出机制，在多个推理基准上平均减少 30.2%–39.8% 的推理 token 消耗，同时保持与完整长度推理相当的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型（LRMs）的长链推理在中间答案跨步骤稳定后变得低效：额外的推理步骤边际收益极低，却带来巨大的推理成本。现有基于置信度或熵的提前退出方法难以有效捕捉推理稳定性；而基于一致性的方法依赖多步轨迹一致性，需要顺序评估，导致退出延迟。因此需要在效率与可靠性之间实现更好的平衡。

Method: SABER 通过在中间推理状态周围构建简单而有效的语义扰动来形成对抗分支，并采用轻量级探测来估计各分支的可能最终结果，无需完整轨迹展开。当探测结果在分支间保持一致时，SABER 提前退出；否则继续推理。整个过程无需额外训练。

Result: 在多个推理基准测试和模型架构上，SABER 平均减少推理 token 消耗 30.2%–39.8%，同时保持与完整长度推理具有竞争力的准确率。

Conclusion: 对抗分支探测为检测推理稳定性提供了一种免训练的方案，在保持推理可靠性的同时显著降低了推理成本，为大推理模型的高效推理提供了实用可行的新途径。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong reasoning capabilities, yet long-chain reasoning becomes inefficient once the intermediate answer stabilizes across reasoning steps: additional reasoning yields little marginal benefit while incurring substantial inference cost. Existing early-exit methods based on confidence or entropy poorly capture reasoning stability, while consistency-based approaches rely on multi-step trajectory agreement, requiring sequential evaluations that delay exit. To better balance efficiency and reliability, we propose SABER, a training-free framework for stability-aware early exit via adversarial branch probing. SABER constructs simple yet effective semantic perturbations around intermediate reasoning states to form adversarial branches, and applies lightweight probing to estimate their likely final outcomes without full trajectory rollouts. When the probed outcomes remain consistent across branches, SABER exits early; otherwise, it continues reasoning. Experiments across multiple reasoning benchmarks and model architectures show that SABER reduces reasoning token consumption by 30.2\%--39.8\% on average while maintaining competitive accuracy with full-length reasoning.

</details>


### [224] [AERA: Adaptive Evidence Residual Allocation for Efficient Test-Time Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27964)
*Ziming Wang,Ivor Tsang,Hangwei Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Adaptive Evidence Residual Allocation (AERA)，一种自适应推理控制器，通过学习判断额外计算能否从当前检查点证据中恢复更优答案，实现按问题难度动态分配推理预算，在 GSM8K 和 GPQA Diamond 上大幅降低计算开销的同时保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展（test-time scaling）方法为每个问题分配相同推理预算，造成计算浪费；已有自适应停止方法（基于置信度、一致性、答案稳定性等）隐含假设"当前证据越强→无需进一步计算"，但本文指出该假设会失效：检查点级别的正确性呈非单调演化，可观察证据可能在答案崩溃前增强、或在答案恢复前减弱。

Method: AERA 是一个序贯控制器，使用离线监督信号（未来检查点正确性）训练，但推理时完全不可用。核心步骤：1) 用答案分布、时间特征、重解特征、语义特征和计算特征对累积响应前缀进行表征；2) 训练一个二分类器学习"当前检查点的证据是否预示额外计算能恢复更优答案"；3) 推理时重复执行 stop/allocate 决策，决定是否生成下一个响应块。关键设计：监督标签来自离线阶段的未来正确性，推理时仅使用可观察的当前证据特征。

Result: 在 GSM8K 和 GPQA Diamond 上，AERA 识别出问题特定的残余改进机会并显著减少推理计算。在300道未使用的 GSM8K 题目上的固定阈值增量生成评估中：AERA 达到 92.61% 准确率（对比 128 个响应的基线 93.01%），同时将完成 token 数减少 95.99%。

Conclusion: 自适应推理的核心应是估计计算的"未来价值"（即额外计算是否可能改善答案），而非将当前置信度等同于正确性。AERA 通过残余分配框架，以极小的精度损失换取近96%的计算节省，为高效测试时扩展提供了新范式。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves language-model reasoning by generating additional candidate solutions, but allocating the same inference budget to every problem is computationally wasteful. Existing adaptive stopping methods commonly rely on confidence, agreement, or answer stability, implicitly assuming that stronger current evidence indicates that further computation is unnecessary. We show that this assumption can fail: checkpoint-level correctness evolves non-monotonically, and observable evidence may strengthen before an answer collapses or weaken before it recovers. Motivated by this mismatch, we introduce Adaptive Evidence Residual Allocation (AERA), a sequential controller that learns whether additional computation is likely to recover a better answer from checkpoint-observable evidence. AERA characterizes cumulative response prefixes using answer-distribution, temporal, re-solving, semantic, and compute features, and repeatedly decides whether to stop or allocate the next response block. Future checkpoint correctness is used only to construct offline supervision and is never available to the controller at inference time. Across GSM8K and GPQA Diamond, AERA identifies question-specific residual opportunities while substantially reducing inference computation. In a frozen-threshold incremental-generation evaluation on 300 untouched GSM8K questions, AERA achieves 92.61% accuracy versus 93.01% with 128 responses while reducing completion tokens by 95.99%. These results suggest that adaptive reasoning should estimate the future value of computation rather than equating present confidence with correctness.

</details>


### [225] [openJiuwen: Beyond Static Harnesses for Long-Horizon Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27969)
*openJiuwen Team,Tao Yu,Xinyu Zhang,Qianqian Chen,Xiaoneng Xiang,Chia Kwangyang,Xingchen Huang,Ran Chen,Yangkai Ding,Zheng Wang,Yeo Boon Hong,Bingzheng Gan,Enrui Hu,Shuo Cheng,Deyang Li,Ruifeng Shi,Hongbo Wang,Qi Ye,Xuefeng Jin,Zhangchun Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: openJiuwen 是一个开源的长时程编码代理框架（agent harness），通过"结构化可组合性"（Rail-based 能力组合）和"运行时自适应性"（基于动态证据的自适应决策）两大设计原则，在 SWE-bench Verified 和 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上分别达到 82.6% 和 87.19%，超越官方排行榜最强结果 3.4 和 3.39 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程编码代理在进化中的仓库状态上运行，且日益依赖异构能力、委托子代理和多代理协同。这给代理框架（harness）带来两个互补挑战：
1. **结构化可组合性（Structural Composability）**：开发者需要在不重复重建编排逻辑的前提下，组合能力、重构执行逻辑并扩展日益复杂的代理系统。
2. **运行时自适应性（Runtime Adaptivity）**：复杂编码任务持续产生新证据（语义诊断、执行结果、任务进度、上下文相关性变化等），这些证据应动态影响后续运行时决策。
现有框架通常在这两个维度上不够兼顾——要么编排灵活性不足，要么运行时决策僵化，无法充分利用任务执行过程中不断涌现的信息。

Method: openJiuwen 的核心方法包括两个层面：

**1. 结构化可组合性——Rail-based 能力组合**
- 提供**共享执行基底（shared execution substrate）**，为不同代理类型提供统一执行语义。
- 通过 **Rail 机制**实现能力组合，横跨三种执行模式：
  - 单代理（single agent）
  - 委托子代理（delegated sub-agents）
  - Swarm Flow（群体代理流）
- 开发者可在统一执行语义下构建复杂代理框架，无需重复编写编排逻辑。

**2. 运行时自适应性——基于固定模型策略的框架层自适应**
- 围绕**固定的模型策略（fixed model policy）**，在**框架控制层**实现运行时自适应。
- 将任务执行过程中涌现的动态证据（诊断信息、执行反馈、上下文变化等）注入运行时决策，动态调整：
  - 上下文选择
  - 反馈机制
  - 任务控制流
- 使得后续执行步骤能够根据最新证据做出更优决策，从而提高任务完成质量。

**系统评测**：在 SWE-bench Verified 和 Terminal-Bench 2.1 两个基准上进行系统性评估。

Result: - **SWE-bench Verified**：82.6%，超越官方排行榜最强点估计 **3.4 个百分点**。
- **Terminal-Bench 2.1**：87.19%，超越官方排行榜最强点估计 **3.39 个百分点**。
- 结果表明 openJiuwen 在复杂编码任务上取得显著领先，同时其框架设计兼具可组合性与自适应能力。

Conclusion: openJiuwen 证明了：将**结构化可组合性**（使开发者能够灵活构建和扩展代理系统）与**运行时自适应性**（使执行过程能根据动态证据实时调整决策）相结合，可以在保持框架灵活性的同时实现强性能表现。这一设计为构建高效、可扩展的长时程编码代理系统提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Long-horizon coding agents operate over evolving repository states while increasingly relying on heterogeneous capabilities, delegated agents, and multi-agent coordination. These trends pose two complementary challenges for the agent harness. First, developers need to compose capabilities, reconfigure execution logic, and scale increasingly complex agent systems without repeatedly rebuilding orchestration. Second, complex coding tasks continuously produce new evidence---such as semantic diagnostics, execution outcomes, task progress, and changing context relevance---that should dynamically influence subsequent runtime decisions. We characterize these challenges as Structural Composability and Runtime Adaptivity. We present openJiuwen, an open-source harness designed for both developer composability and adaptive task execution. openJiuwen provides a shared execution substrate and Rail-based capability composition across single agents, delegated sub-agents, and Swarm Flow, enabling developers to construct sophisticated agent harnesses under common execution semantics. It further adapts framework-controlled runtime decisions around a fixed model policy, allowing evolving evidence to dynamically affect context, feedback, and task control toward successful completion. We systematically evaluate openJiuwen on SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 2.1, where it achieves 82.6% and 87.19%, respectively, exceeding the strongest selected official-leaderboard point estimates by 3.4 and 3.39 percentage points. These results show that openJiuwen achieves strong performance on complex coding tasks while providing a composable and adaptive harness design.

</details>


### [226] [Learning from Hard Prompts: Difficulty-aware Advantage Amplification in Dynamic Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27982)
*Siyuan Gan,Yuhan Li,Xiran Wang,Linjian Meng,Boyan Wang,Zhen Zhao,Jing Huo,Lei Bai,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DA3PO（Difficulty-aware Advantage Amplification Policy Optimization），指出DAPO中Dynamic Sampling虽提升准确率但降低训练效率，并通过Direct Advantage Amplification（DAA）在仅少于30行代码的改动下显著超越了GRPO及其经典变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAPO的Dynamic Sampling通过过滤"全对"或"全错"的prompt来消除零策略梯度以提升训练稳定性，但其理论分析表明：Dynamic Sampling在困难prompt上不对称地放大advantage——错误响应被放大的程度大于正确响应。这导致模型倾向于"回避"已观测到的错误响应，而非有效利用困难prompt上难得的正确响应，从而降低训练效率。作者旨在修复这一缺陷，在保持Dynamic Sampling准确率收益的同时恢复训练效率。

Method: 提出Direct Advantage Amplification（DAA）：针对Dynamic Sampling筛选出的困难prompt，DAA有目的地放大那些"难以采样到的正确响应"的advantage。这使得模型在训练时能够有效利用这些稀有但宝贵的正样本信号，而非仅被动避免负样本。将DAA集成到DAPO中，形成DA3PO。实现上仅在DAPO基础上新增少于30行代码，修改成本极低。

Result: 实验表明DA3PO在准确率上显著优于GRPO及其他经典GRPO变体（如DAPO本身），在保持准确率增益的同时改善了训练效率。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了一个GRPO变体中被忽视的训练效率问题：Dynamic Sampling的过滤策略虽然消除了零梯度但引入了advantage放大不对称性，使模型学习偏向"避免错误"而非"学习正确"。通过轻量级的DAA机制（<30行代码）解决该问题，DA3PO在不增加复杂度的前提下同时获得准确率与训练效率的双重提升，说明对advantage放大方向的精细化控制是RL策略优化中的重要设计维度。

Abstract: Decoupled Clip and Dynamic Sampling Policy Optimization (DAPO) is a prominent variant of Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). DAPO introduces several improvements over GRPO. Among these, Dynamic Sampling contributes the most to DAPO's accuracy gains relative to GRPO. To improve accuracy, Dynamic Sampling enhances training stability by eliminating zero policy gradients from zero advantages. Specifically, it avoids such zero gradients by filtering out prompts where sampled responses are either entirely correct or incorrect. However, our theoretical analysis shows that Dynamic Sampling decrease training efficiency as it cannot effectively utilize hard-to-sample correct responses on hard prompts. Formally, it asymmetrically amplifies the advantages of distinct responses to the same prompts. On hard prompts, incorrect responses undergo greater amplification than correct ones. This leads the model to avoid generating the observed incorrect responses rather than capitalizing on the hard-to-sample correct ones on hard prompts, resulting in low training efficiency. To improve training efficiency, we propose Direct Advantage Amplification (DAA), which amplifies the advantages of hard-to-sample correct responses on hard prompts, as obtained by Dynamic Sampling. This ensures that, when Dynamic Sampling is used, these hard-to-sample responses can be effectively capitalized on, implying higher training efficiency. By integrating DAA into DAPO, we obtain Difficulty-aware Advantage Amplification Policy Optimization (DA3PO), which is implemented with fewer than 30 lines of code from DAPO. Experiments show that DA3PO significantly outperforms GRPO and other classical GRPO variants.

</details>


### [227] [When Evidence Shapes Collaboration: Knowledge-Conditioned Topology Generation for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27984)
*Yangxiao Jiang,Jiarun Fan,Mingcong Xu,Yanxi Guo,Jiwen Feng,Shanqing Xu,Mengchen Qian,Wei Chen,Xiaojin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: K-GAT（Knowledge-Guided Agent Topology Generator）是一种神经-符号框架，将多智能体系统的协作拓扑生成建模为知识条件化的结构学习问题，将外部证据直接嵌入自回归图生成过程，在知识密集型基准任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统（MAS）的协作拓扑生成方法主要依赖大语言模型的参数化知识，外部检索仅作为被动工具使用，而非协作结构的显式决定因素。这导致"结构-知识不对齐"问题——在知识密集型任务中出现冗余交互或验证不足的现象。

Method: 提出K-GAT框架，将协作拓扑设计建模为知识条件化的结构学习问题，采用神经-符号方法，将外部证据（检索知识）直接整合进自回归图生成过程，使知识显式地驱动协作拓扑的生成，而非仅在推理阶段被动调用。

Result: 在专家级GPQA数据集上，K-GAT相比LLM-Debate基线准确率提升+15.7%，同时消耗的计算token不到基线的一半，展示了显著的效率与有效性提升。在多个知识密集型基准上的广泛实验进一步验证了该方法的优势。

Conclusion: 通过将外部知识直接纳入拓扑生成过程（而非仅在推理阶段被动检索），K-GAT有效解决了结构-知识不对齐问题，实现了精度与效率的双重提升，表明知识条件化的结构学习是构建高效多智能体协作系统的关键方向。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) have recently moved from static workflows toward dynamically generated collaboration topologies. However, existing topology generation methods rely primarily on the parametric knowledge of large language models, with external search or retrieval used only as a reactive tool rather than an explicit determinant of collaboration structure. This leads to structure-knowledge misalignment, where systems exhibit redundant interactions or insufficient verification in knowledge-intensive tasks. We propose K-GAT (Knowledge-Guided Agent Topology Generator), a neuro-symbolic framework that formulates collaboration topology design as a knowledge-conditioned structure learning problem, integrating external evidence directly into autoregressive graph generation. Extensive experiments on knowledge-intensive benchmarks demonstrate K-GAT's efficiency and effectiveness: notably on the expert-level GPQA dataset, K-GAT outperforms the LLM-Debate baseline by a substantial margin of +15.7% in accuracy, while consuming less than half the computational tokens.

</details>


### [228] [GOD: Govern, Observe, and Direct - A Real-Time Control Room for Agent Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27992)
*Yige Luo,Ran Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GOD 是一个本地优先（local-first）的生成式智能体系统控制面板，通过统一的浏览器命令界面提供对多智能体运行过程的实时干预、空间回放检视和可移植实验包打包能力，使操作者能够"追问为什么"、测试小干预并方便地共享实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式智能体系统的运行产生大量代理、位置、消息、命令和模型调用，但现有工具通常只给出两种极端输出：完整的回放录像或原始日志。这使得操作者难以追问某个智能体为何做出特定移动、难以对运行施加小型干预进行测试、也难以将实验结果打包给其他研究者复现。缺乏一个将实时控制与回放证据统一起来、且能分离运行时状态与实验定义的中间层工具。

Method: GOD 的核心贡献是"命令-制品环路"（command and artifact loop）：实时控件与回放证据共享同一套操作员命令模型（operator command model），使干预指令与回放查询具有统一的语义；同时通过"包契约"（package contracts）将场景（scenario）、地图（map）和画像（profile）数据与本地运行时状态分离。系统由以下组件构成：(1) 设置向导（setup wizard）用于快速配置实验；(2) Agent Studio 用于定义和编辑智能体；(3) Map Studio 用于编辑空间环境；(4) 空间回放界面（spatial replay interface）用于可视化检视运行状态；(5) Ask 命令用于向运行中的系统发出定向问题；(6) Intervene 命令用于注入干预操作；(7) 可移植的实验包、地图包和智能体包，支持跨机器共享。公开发布包含托管的 Smallville 风格回放、PKU 回放、开源仓库和可下载的实验包。

Result: 在 15 个已完成的运行槽位上进行评估。在 14 个干预运行中，84 项目标智能体检查中有 78 项（约 92.9%）正确记录了被命令的目标位置；在状态查询方面，182 个状态回答中有 169 个（约 92.9%）匹配了保存的位置或动作字符串。评估还展示了托管回放（Smallville 风格与 PKU 场景）的可用性以及开源仓库与可下载包的完整性。

Conclusion: GOD 证明了一种务实的本地优先路径，使操作者能够在统一的命令模型下对生成式智能体社会进行实时干预、事后追溯检视和实验打包共享。命令-制品环路与包契约的设计将运行时状态与实验定义解耦，提升了可复现性与可分享性。约 93% 的干预与状态查询准确率表明该工具在实际操作场景中具有较好的可靠性，为生成式智能体研究的可解释性和协作调试提供了基础设施层面的补充。

Abstract: Generative-agent systems are easier to start than to inspect. A run can contain many agents, locations, messages, commands, and model calls, yet the operator often gets either a finished replay or raw logs. That makes it hard to ask why an agent moved, test a small intervention, or package a run for another researcher. GOD is a local-first control room for agent societies. From the same browser workflow, an operator can issue targeted questions or interventions and inspect the resulting replay state. The system combines a setup wizard, Agent Studio, Map Studio, a spatial replay interface, Ask and Intervene commands, and portable experiment, map, and agent packs. Its technical contribution is the command and artifact loop: live controls and replay evidence share the same operator command model, while package contracts separate scenario, map, and profile data from local runtime state. The public release includes hosted Smallville-style and PKU replays, the open-source repository, and downloadable packs. We evaluate this path on 15 completed run slots. Across the 14 intervention runs, 78 of 84 target-agent checks recorded the commanded destination, and 169 of 182 state answers matched a saved location or action string.

</details>


### [229] [Should I Use This Synthetic Dataset for Training? How to Test with Minimal Real Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27996)
*Zhenyu Tao,Wei Xu,Xiaohu You,Petar Popovski,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 aeSFT（adaptive e-process sign-flip test），一个双重自适应的检验程序，用于以尽可能少的真实测试样本判断合成数据增强是否真正提升了模型在真实分布上的表现，同时提供 anytime-valid 的 I 类错误控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生（DT）和学习型世界模型被广泛用于生成合成数据以补充工程系统中稀缺的真实训练集。然而由于不可避免的 sim-to-real gap，合成数据增强可能无法（甚至可能恶化）模型在真实数据分布上的性能。由此引出一个核心决策问题：给定真实数据集、候选合成数据集和固定学习算法，判断"使用增强数据训练"是否真的能提升总体（population-level）性能——同时消耗尽可能少的真实测试数据。

Method: **两种检验框架：**
1. **直接均值差异检验**：直接检验两个训练模型之间的平均损失差异。
2. **对称性配对检验**：基于配对损失差异的对称性进行检验，以更强的零假设换取更快的证据积累。

**aeSFT（adaptive e-process sign-flip test）：** 针对第二种框架，提出一个**双重自适应**程序：
- **自适应蒙特卡洛 sign-flip 轮数**：根据证据积累动态调整计算开销；
- **自适应真实测试样本量**：边检验边决定消耗多少真实测试数据，无需预先指定测试集大小。

**统计保证：** 基于 e-process 构建，提供 anytime-valid 的 I 类错误控制，即在任何停止时刻 Type-I 错误率均不超过目标水平。

Result: 在三个实验任务上验证：
1. **合成数据分类任务**
2. **DT 辅助的无线包调度任务**
3. **无线电地图预测任务**

核心结果：
- aeSFT 用**显著更少的真实测试样本**即可识别有用的合成数据，相比基于均值的序贯检验大幅降低测试数据消耗；
- 统计功效（power）**匹配**固定样本 sign-flip 检验和配对 t 检验；
- 在整个过程中 **假阳性率始终低于目标水平**。

Conclusion: aeSFT 为"合成数据增强是否值得"这一工程决策问题提供了一个统计上可靠且实际高效的解决方案。其双重自适应设计（同时优化计算成本与测试数据消耗）使得在不预设测试集规模的前提下，依然能保证 anytime-valid 的错误控制，并在多种工程场景中表现出优于现有方法的数据效率与统计功效平衡。

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) and learned world models are increasingly used to generate synthetic data that augment the scarce real datasets available for training artificial intelligence (AI) models in engineering systems. Owing to the inevitable simulation-to-reality (sim-to-real) gap, however, augmentation may fail to improve the performance of the trained model on the real data distribution. This paper addresses the resulting decision problem: Given a real dataset, a candidate synthetic dataset, and a fixed learning algorithm, decide whether training on the augmented dataset improves the true, population-level performance, while consuming as few real test data points as possible. Two formulations are considered: a direct test on the mean loss difference between the two trained models, and a symmetry-based test on the paired loss difference, which trades a stronger null assumption for faster evidence accumulation. For the latter, we introduce the {adaptive e-process sign-flip test} (aeSFT), a doubly adaptive procedure that adapts both the number of Monte Carlo sign-flip rounds, and hence the computational cost, and the amount of real test data consumed. aeSFT yields anytime-valid Type-I error control, with no need to pre-specify the test-set size. Experiments on a synthetic-data classification task, a DT-aided wireless packet-scheduling task, and a radio-map prediction task show that aeSFT identifies useful synthetic data using substantially fewer real test samples than mean-based sequential testing, matches the power of fixed-sample sign-flip testing and the paired $t$-test, while keeping the false-positive rate below the target level.

</details>


### [230] [Automated Analysis Framework for Multilingual Climate-Health Literature Based on Multi-Agent Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27998)
*Yuze Sun,Shihui Zhang,Jiancheng Pan,Yunjia Ye,Wentao Luo,Jiahao Li,Quan Zhang,Wenjia Cai,Xiaomeng Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向跨学科气候-健康领域的多智能体大语言模型自动化文献分析框架，实现了多语科学文献从筛选、结构化信息提取到标准化整合的全流程自动化，在32,642篇中英双语语料上核心信息提取F1达0.92，为大规模证据挖掘提供了技术支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨学科、多语科学文献数量快速增长，而传统手工分析与单算法方法存在效率低、可扩展性差、领域适应性不足三大瓶颈，难以满足气候-健康等典型跨学科领域的大规模文献分析需求。

Method: 构建以中央协调模块为核心的多智能体LLM框架，部署三个专用智能体：①文档评估智能体（文献筛选）；②信息提取智能体（结构化数据抽取）；③分析审查智能体（模拟领域专家思维进行审查与整合）。框架还引入了四层幻觉控制策略与人工验证流程，以确保输出结果的准确性与可靠性。

Result: 基于涵盖中国1993–2023年32,642篇气候-健康中英双语论文语料进行验证：核心信息提取F1分数达0.92；成功完成2,012个城市-文献关联对的提取与标准化，验证了框架在大规模、跨语言文献分析任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该多智能体LLM框架为气候-健康领域的多语科学文献分析提供了高效、可扩展且可靠的自动化解决方案，有效克服了传统方法的局限性，为跨学科大规模证据挖掘奠定了技术基础。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of interdisciplinary and multilingual scientific literature has left traditional manual analysis and single-algorithm methods plagued by low efficiency, poor scalability, and insufficient domain adaptability. Targeting the literature analysis needs of the typical interdisciplinary climate-health field, this study proposes a multi-agent large language model automated analysis framework for multilingual scientific literature, which realizes full-process automation covering literature screening, structured information extraction, and standardized integration. With a central coordination module as the core, the framework deploys three dedicated agents for document evaluation, information extraction, and analytical review to mimic the literature analysis thinking of domain experts, and adopts a four-layer hallucination control strategy together with a manual verification procedure to ensure the accuracy and reliability of analytical outcomes. Validated on a bilingual Chinese-English corpus of 32,642 climate-health papers covering China from 1993 to 2023, the framework achieves an F1 score of 0.92 in core information extraction, and completes the extraction and standardization of 2,012 city-literature association pairs, offering effective technical support for large-scale evidence mining in the climate-health research domain.

</details>


### [231] [PhenoIntel: A Lifecycle-Aligned Multi-Agent Web Application for Verified, Accessible Plant Phenotype Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27999)
*Narendren S,Soumyashree Kar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhenoIntel 是一个面向植物科学家的浏览器端多智能体表型平台，通过九个分工智能体、阶段间独立校验、按模型族匹配的自适应不确定性量化（保形预测/检测置信度/蒙特卡洛 Dropout），以及 FAIR 合规的来源追溯，解决了现有对话式表型工具的核心痛点——分析失败被静默报告为有效测量、统计假设未经验证、预测无不确定性估计、依赖专用硬件。其分类模型 Macro F1 达 0.78–0.996，检测模型 mAP@50 达 0.96，计数误差降低 54%，时序模型 F1 达 0.7050；全流程可在无 GPU 的浏览器中运行，并通过 1,200 项自动化测试验证完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式植物表型平台存在系统性缺陷，使其难以满足科研级可靠性要求：

1. **错误静默化**：分析失败被报告为有效测量，而非标记为缺失值——这会导致下游统计和结论基于无效数据。
2. **统计假设缺失**：统计检验在未经检验假设条件下执行，可能产生无效的 p 值或效应量。
3. **不确定性缺失**：预测不带不确定性估计，无法判断结果的可靠边界。
4. **硬件壁垒**：依赖专用硬件限制了使用可及性，阻碍了资源有限实验室的参与。
5. **单一架构低效**：将全部任务交给单个 AI 管理器处理，缺乏阶段性校验和质量控制。

核心动机在于：将完整的机器学习工作流（从图像采集到报告生成）转化为一个既可靠、又用户友好的表型系统，同时不牺牲科学严谨性。

Method: PhenoIntel 采用生命周期对齐的多智能体架构，核心设计要素包括：

**1. 多智能体分阶段架构**
- 九个专业智能体将 ML 工作流划分为多个阶段：图像采集 → 模型选择 → 推理 → 报告生成
- 拒绝"一个 AI 管理器接管全任务"的设计，改为分阶段协作
- 各阶段间设置独立校验（independent checks），确保上游输出一致性才传递至下游

**2. 共享固定结构记录（Shared Fixed-Structure Record）**
- 所有智能体读写同一个共享记录
- 记录结构固定，任一阶段的不一致输出在进入下一阶段前即被捕获拦截
- 这构成了一种类似"数据契约"的防错机制

**3. 自适应不确定性量化**
- 不统一施加同一种不确定性估计方法，而是按模型族匹配：
  - 分类模型 → Conformal Prediction（保形预测）
  - 检测模型 → 检测置信度离散度（detection-confidence spread）
  - 其他模型 → Monte Carlo Dropout
- 质量阈值按作物类型和任务类型自适应调整，而非使用全局统一截止值

**4. 自适模型扩展能力**
- 当不存在合适的预训练模型时，PhenoIntel 可自主提出、验证并集成新模型

**5. 部署架构**
- 纯浏览器端运行，无需 GPU，基于标准硬件
- 运行 1,200 项自动化测试套件以确认完整流程可执行

**6. 模型仓库**
- 涵盖 10 个预训练模型、5 种作物、4 种成像模态

**7. 数据溯源**
- 所有结果附带 FAIR 合规的来源记录（Findable, Accessible, Interoperable, Reusable）

Result: | 指标 | 数值 |
|---|---|
| 模型仓库规模 | 10 个训练模型 / 5 种作物 / 4 种成像模态 |
| 分类模型 Macro F1 | 0.78 – 0.996 |
| 目标检测模型 mAP@50 | 0.96 |
| 检测计数误差降低 | 相比未优化基线降低 54% |
| 时序模型 held-out Macro F1 | 0.7050 |
| 自动化测试覆盖 | 1,200 项测试，确认完整流程执行 |

**关键特性验证：**
- 所有结果附带校准的不确定性（calibrated uncertainty）
- 统计检验经过假设验证
- 数据来源遵循 FAIR 原则（可发现、可访问、可互操作、可重用）

**部署优势：**
- 浏览器端运行，无需 GPU
- 标准硬件即可执行
